+1|// Find and Replace engine for PatchPad
 2|
+3|class FindReplaceEngine {
+4|  constructor() {
+5|    this.query = '';
+6|    this.replaceText = '';
+7|    this.caseSensitive = false;
+8|    this.matches = [];
+9|    this.currentIndex = -1;
+10|  }
+11|
+12|  setQuery(query, caseSensitive = false) {
+13|    this.query = query;
+14|    this.caseSensitive = caseSensitive;
+15|  }
+16|
+17|  setReplaceText(text) {
+18|    this.replaceText = text;
+19|  }
+20|
+21|  /**
+22|   * Search through document lines and find all matches
+23|   * @param {DocumentModel} docModel 
+24|   * @returns {Array<{ start: {line: number, col: number}, end: {line: 
number, col: number} }>}
+25|   */
+26|  updateMatches(docModel) {
+27|    this.matches = [];
+28|    this.currentIndex = -1;
+29|
+30|    if (!this.query) {
+31|      return this.matches;
+32|    }
+33|
+34|    const lines = docModel.lines;
+35|    const query = this.caseSensitive ? this.query : 
this.query.toLowerCase();
+36|    const queryLen = this.query.length;
+37|
+38|    // Handle multiline query
+39|    if (this.query.includes('\n')) {
+40|      const fullText = docModel.getText();
+41|      const targetText = this.caseSensitive ? fullText : 
fullText.toLowerCase();
+42|      let pos = 0;
+43|      while ((pos = targetText.indexOf(query, pos)) !== -1) {
+44|        const startPos = this._charIndexToPos(lines, pos);
+45|        const endPos = this._charIndexToPos(lines, pos + queryLen);
+46|        this.matches.push({ start: startPos, end: endPos });
+47|        pos += queryLen || 1;
+48|      }
+49|    } else {
+50|      // Single line fast path
+51|      for (let lineIdx = 0; lineIdx < lines.length; lineIdx++) {
+52|        const line = lines[lineIdx];
+53|        const lineText = this.caseSensitive ? line : line.toLowerCase();
+54|        let col = 0;
+55|        while ((col = lineText.indexOf(query, col)) !== -1) {
+56|          this.matches.push({
+57|            start: { line: lineIdx, col },
+58|            end: { line: lineIdx, col: col + queryLen }
+59|          });
+60|          col += queryLen || 1;
+61|        }
+62|      }
+63|    }
+64|
+65|    return this.matches;
+66|  }
+67|
+68|  _charIndexToPos(lines, charIndex) {
+69|    let count = 0;
+70|    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
+71|      const lineLen = lines[i].length + 1; // +1 for \n
+72|      if (count + lineLen > charIndex) {
+73|        return { line: i, col: charIndex - count };
+74|      }
+75|      count += lineLen;
+76|    }
+77|    const lastLine = lines.length - 1;
+78|    return { line: lastLine, col: lines[lastLine].length };
+79|  }
+80|
+81|  /**
+82|   * Find index of match closest to or at current cursor position
+83|   * @param {{ line: number, col: number }} pos 
+84|   * @param {DocumentModel} docModel 
+85|   */
+86|  findNearestMatchIndex(pos, docModel) {
+87|    if (this.matches.length === 0) return -1;
+88|    for (let i = 0; i < this.matches.length; i++) {
+89|      const m = this.matches[i];
+90|      if (docModel.comparePositions(m.start, pos) >= 0) {
+91|        return i;
+92|      }
+93|    }
+94|    return 0; // Wrap to first
+95|  }
+96|
+97|  nextMatch() {
+98|    if (this.matches.length === 0) {
+99|      this.currentIndex = -1;
+100|      return null;
+101|    }
+102|    if (this.currentIndex === -1) {
+103|      this.currentIndex = 0;
+104|    } else {
+105|      this.currentIndex = (this.currentIndex + 1) % this.matches.length;
+106|    }
+107|    return this.matches[this.currentIndex];
+108|  }
+109|
+110|  prevMatch() {
+111|    if (this.matches.length === 0) {
+112|      this.currentIndex = -1;
+113|      return null;
+114|    }
+115|    if (this.currentIndex <= 0) {
+116|      this.currentIndex = this.matches.length - 1;
+117|    } else {
+118|      this.currentIndex = this.currentIndex - 1;
+119|    }
+120|    return this.matches[this.currentIndex];
+121|  }
+122|
+123|  getCurrentMatch() {
+124|    if (this.currentIndex >= 0 && this.currentIndex < this.matches.length) 
{
+125|      return this.matches[this.currentIndex];
+126|    }
+127|    return null;
+128|  }
+129|
+130|  /**
+131|   * Replace the currently selected match
+132|   * @param {DocumentModel} docModel 
+133|   * @param {string} [replacement] 
+134|   */
+135|  replaceCurrent(docModel, replacement = this.replaceText) {
+136|    const current = this.getCurrentMatch();
+137|    if (!current) return null;
+138|
+139|    const beforeText = docModel.getText();
+140|    const res = docModel.replaceRange(current.start, current.end, 
replacement);
+141|    const afterText = docModel.getText();
+142|
+143|    // Recompute matches after replacement
+144|    this.updateMatches(docModel);
+145|
+146|    // Keep current index or clamp
+147|    if (this.currentIndex >= this.matches.length) {
+148|      this.currentIndex = 0;
+149|    }
+150|
+151|    const nextMatch = this.getCurrentMatch();
+152|
+153|    return {
+154|      beforeText,
+155|      afterText,
+156|      replacedRange: { start: current.start, end: res.newEndPos },
+157|      nextMatch,
+158|      selection: {
+159|        anchor: current.start,
+160|        head: res.newEndPos
+161|      }
+162|    };
+163|  }
+164|
+165|  /**
+166|   * Replace all occurrences in the document
+167|   * @param {DocumentModel} docModel 
+168|   * @param {string} [replacement] 
+169|   */
+170|  replaceAll(docModel, replacement = this.replaceText) {
+171|    if (!this.query || this.matches.length === 0) {
+172|      return { count: 0, beforeText: docModel.getText(), afterText: 
docModel.getText() };
+173|    }
+174|
+175|    const beforeText = docModel.getText();
+176|    const count = this.matches.length;
+177|
+178|    // Apply replacements backwards from last match to first match
+179|    for (let i = this.matches.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+180|      const m = this.matches[i];
+181|      docModel.replaceRange(m.start, m.end, replacement);
+182|    }
+183|
+184|    const afterText = docModel.getText();
+185|    this.updateMatches(docModel);
+186|    this.currentIndex = -1;
+187|
+188|    return {
+189|      count,
+190|      beforeText,
+191|      afterText
+192|    };
+193|  }
+194|}
+195|
+196|if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
+197|  module.exports = { FindReplaceEngine };
+198|}

Alright, time to get down to brass tacks and really put this `find-replace.js` 
module through its paces in Node. It's crucial to cover all the bases to ensure 
robust behavior, especially since seemingly simple functions can hide surprising
edge cases.

First, I need to make sure the core functionality is rock solid: accurate 
*finding* of all occurrences. That seems obvious, but I'll need to really test 
the different scenarios – case sensitivity, regular expressions, partial 
matches, and so on, to be absolutely certain it's behaving as expected.

Then, the navigation is critical. Testing that the "Next" and "Previous" 
functionality correctly wraps around the document when reaching the end or 
beginning is vital. No buggy navigation allowed!

Replacing, I will make sure the "Replace Current" function behaves as expected, 
that it replaces only the current instance. But the big test will come with 
"Replace All". It's easy to make mistakes in a mass replacement, so I’ll need to
put it through rigorous tests to ensure it modifies all the intended occurrences
without unintended consequences.

Finally, an important aspect to confirm is how the "Single Undo" functionality 
works when reverting a "Replace All". I'll need to make certain that the undo 
operates correctly and reverses the mass replacement, not leaving the document 
in a corrupt or incomplete state.

navigator.platform.toUpperCase().indexOf('MAC') >= 0;\n    const ctrlOrCmd = 
isMac ? e.metaKey : e.ctrlKey;\n\n    // Shortcuts\n    if (ctrlOrCmd && 
!e.altKey && !e.shiftKey) {\n      if (e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S') {\n      
e.preventDefault();\n        if (this.onSaveRequest) this.onSaveRequest();\n    
return;\n      }\n      if (e.key === 'f' || e.key === 'F') {\n        
e.preventDefault();\n        if (this.onFindRequest) this.onFindRequest();\n    
return;\n      }\n      if (e.key === 'z' || e.key === 'Z') {\n        
e.preventDefault();\n        this.performUndo();\n        return;\n      }\n    
if (e.key === 'y' || e.key === 'Y') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        
this.performRedo();\n        return;\n      }\n      if (e.key === 'a' || e.key 
=== 'A') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        this.selectAll();\n        
return;\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (ctrlOrCmd && e.shiftKey && (e.key === 'z' || 
e.key === 'Z')) {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      this.performRedo();\n      
return;\n    }\n\n    // Escape\n    if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n      
e.preventDefault();\n      if (this.onEscape) this.onEscape();\n      return;\n 
}\n\n    // Tab / Shift+Tab (Indentation)\n    if (e.key === 'Tab') {\n      
e.preventDefault();\n      if (e.shiftKey) {\n        this.outdent();\n      } 
else {\n        this.indent();\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Enter / 
Newline\n    if (e.key === 'Enter') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      
this.insertNewline();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Backspace\n    if (e.key 
=== 'Backspace') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      
this.deleteBackward(ctrlOrCmd || e.altKey);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // 
Delete\n    if (e.key === 'Delete') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      
this.deleteForward(ctrlOrCmd || e.altKey);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // 
Navigation Keys\n    if (['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 
'Home', 'End', 'PageUp', 'PageDown'].includes(e.key)) {\n      
e.preventDefault();\n      this._handleNavigation(e.key, e.shiftKey, ctrlOrCmd 
|| e.altKey, ctrlOrCmd);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Normal Character 
Insertion fallback (if beforeinput not fired)\n    if (!ctrlOrCmd && !e.altKey 
&& e.key.length === 1) {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      
this.insertTextAtCarets(e.key, 'typing');\n    }\n  }\n\n  
_handleNavigation(key, isShift, isWordMod, isDocMod) {\n    const isMac = 
navigator.platform.toUpperCase().indexOf('MAC') >= 0;\n\n    this.selections = 
this.selections.map(sel => {\n      let head = { ...sel.head };\n      let 
anchor = isShift ? { ...sel.anchor } : { ...head };\n\n      switch (key) {\n   
case 'ArrowLeft': {\n          if (!isShift && 
!this.doc.isPositionsEqual(sel.anchor, sel.head)) {\n            // Collapse to 
start of selection\n            const norm = this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel);\n
head = { ...norm.start };\n            anchor = { ...head };\n          } else 
if (isWordMod) {\n            head = this.doc.getPrevWordPos(head);\n           
if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };\n          } else {\n            head = 
this.doc.getPrevGraphemePos(head);\n            if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head
};\n          }\n          this.desiredCol = head.col;\n          break;\n      
}\n        case 'ArrowRight': {\n          if (!isShift && 
!this.doc.isPositionsEqual(sel.anchor, sel.head)) {\n            // Collapse to 
end of selection\n            const norm = this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel);\n  
head = { ...norm.end };\n            anchor = { ...head };\n          } else if 
(isWordMod) {\n            head = this.doc.getNextWordPos(head);\n            if
(!isShift) anchor = { ...head };\n          } else {\n            head = 
this.doc.getNextGraphemePos(head);\n            if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head
};\n          }\n          this.desiredCol = head.col;\n          break;\n      
}\n        case 'ArrowUp': {\n          if (isDocMod) {\n            head = { 
line: 0, col: 0 };\n          } else if (head.line > 0) {\n            const 
targetLine = head.line - 1;\n            const col = 
Math.min(this.doc.getLineLength(targetLine), this.desiredCol);\n            head
= { line: targetLine, col };\n          } else {\n            head = { line: 0, 
col: 0 };\n          }\n          if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };\n         
break;\n        }\n        case 'ArrowDown': {\n          if (isDocMod) {\n     
const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;\n            head = { line: 
lastLine, col: this.doc.getLineLength(lastLine) };\n          } else if 
(head.line < this.doc.getLineCount() - 1) {\n            const targetLine = 
head.line + 1;\n            const col = 
Math.min(this.doc.getLineLength(targetLine), this.desiredCol);\n            head
= { line: targetLine, col };\n          } else {\n            const lastLine = 
this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;\n            head = { line: lastLine, col: 
this.doc.getLineLength(lastLine) };\n          }\n          if (!isShift) anchor
= { ...head };\n          break;\n        }\n        case 'Home': {\n          
if (isDocMod) {\n            head = { line: 0, col: 0 };\n          } else {\n  
head = { line: head.line, col: 0 };\n          }\n          this.desiredCol = 
head.col;\n          if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };\n          break;\n    
}\n        case 'End': {\n          if (isDocMod) {\n            const lastLine 
= this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;\n            head = { line: lastLine, col: 
this.doc.getLineLength(lastLine) };\n          } else {\n            head = { 
line: head.line, col: this.doc.getLineLength(head.line) };\n          }\n       
this.desiredCol = head.col;\n          if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };\n    
break;\n        }\n        case 'PageUp': {\n          const jump = Math.max(1, 
Math.floor(this.scrollContainer.clientHeight / this.lineHeight));\n          
const targetLine = Math.max(0, head.line - jump);\n          head = { line: 
targetLine, col: Math.min(this.doc.getLineLength(targetLine), this.desiredCol) 
};\n          if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };\n          break;\n        }\n
case 'PageDown': {\n          const jump = Math.max(1, 
Math.floor(this.scrollContainer.clientHeight / this.lineHeight));\n          
const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;\n          const targetLine = 
Math.min(lastLine, head.line + jump);\n          head = { line: targetLine, col:
Math.min(this.doc.getLineLength(targetLine), this.desiredCol) };\n          if 
(!isShift) anchor = { ...head };\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n\n      
return { anchor, head };\n    });\n\n    this.render();\n    
this._emitCursorChange();\n    this.scrollToCursor();\n  }\n\n  selectAll() {\n 
const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;\n    this.selections = [{\n      
anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 },\n      head: { line: lastLine, col: 
this.doc.getLineLength(lastLine) }\n    }];\n    this.render();\n    
this._emitCursorChange();\n  }\n\n  _handleCopy(e) {\n    const textToCopy = 
this.selections.map(s => 
this.doc.getSelectedText(s)).filter(Boolean).join('\\n');\n    if (textToCopy) 
{\n      e.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', textToCopy);\n      
e.preventDefault();\n    }\n  }\n\n  _handleCut(e) {\n    const textToCopy = 
this.selections.map(s => 
this.doc.getSelectedText(s)).filter(Boolean).join('\\n');\n    if (textToCopy) 
{\n      e.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', textToCopy);\n      
this.deleteBackward(false);\n      e.preventDefault();\n    }\n  }\n\n  
_handlePaste(e) {\n    const pasted = e.clipboardData.getData('text/plain');\n  
if (pasted) {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      this.insertTextAtCarets(pasted, 
'paste');\n    }\n  }\n\n  insertTextAtCarets(text, type = 'typing') {\n    
const beforeText = this.doc.getText();\n    const beforeSelections = 
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));\n\n    // Sort selections 
descending by start position to apply edits without shifting coordinates\n    
const sorted = this.selections.map((sel, idx) => ({ sel, idx, norm: 
this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel) }))\n      .sort((a, b) => 
this.doc.comparePositions(b.norm.start, a.norm.start));\n\n    const 
newSelectionsMap = [];\n\n    for (const item of sorted) {\n      const res = 
this.doc.replaceRange(item.norm.start, item.norm.end, text);\n      
newSelectionsMap[item.idx] = {\n        anchor: res.newEndPos,\n        head: 
res.newEndPos\n      };\n    }\n\n    const afterText = this.doc.getText();\n   
this.selections = newSelectionsMap;\n    this.desiredCol = 
this.getPrimarySelection().head.col;\n\n    this.undo.recordAction({\n      
beforeText,\n      afterText,\n      beforeSelections,\n      afterSelections: 
this.selections,\n      type\n    });\n\n    this.render();\n    
this._emitCursorChange();\n    this._emitContentChange(type);\n    
this.scrollToCursor();\n  }\n\n  insertNewline() {\n    
this.insertTextAtCarets('\\n', 'typing');\n  }\n\n  deleteBackward(isWord = 
false) {\n    const beforeText = this.doc.getText();\n    const beforeSelections
= JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));\n\n    const sorted = 
this.selections.map((sel, idx) => ({ sel, idx, norm: 
this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel) }))\n      .sort((a, b) => 
this.doc.comparePositions(b.norm.start, a.norm.start));\n\n    const 
newSelectionsMap = [];\n\n    for (const item of sorted) {\n      let startPos, 
endPos;\n      if (!item.norm.isCollapsed) {\n        startPos = 
item.norm.start;\n        endPos = item.norm.end;\n      } else {\n        
endPos = item.norm.head;\n        if (isWord) {\n          startPos = 
this.doc.getPrevWordPos(endPos);\n        } else {\n          startPos = 
this.doc.getPrevGraphemePos(endPos);\n        }\n      }\n\n      const res = 
this.doc.replaceRange(startPos, endPos, '');\n      newSelectionsMap[item.idx] =
{\n        anchor: res.newEndPos,\n        head: res.newEndPos\n      };\n    
}\n\n    const afterText = this.doc.getText();\n    this.selections = 
newSelectionsMap;\n    this.desiredCol = 
this.getPrimarySelection().head.col;\n\n    this.undo.recordAction({\n      
beforeText,\n      afterText,\n      beforeSelections,\n      afterSelections: 
this.selections,\n      type: 'backspace'\n    });\n\n    this.render();\n    
this._emitCursorChange();\n    this._emitContentChange('delete');\n    
this.scrollToCursor();\n  }\n\n  deleteForward(isWord = false) {\n    const 
beforeText = this.doc.getText();\n    const beforeSelections = 
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));\n\n    const sorted = 
this.selections.map((sel, idx) => ({ sel, idx, norm: 
this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel) }))\n      .sort((a, b) => 
this.doc.comparePositions(b.norm.start, a.norm.start));\n\n    const 
newSelectionsMap = [];\n\n    for (const item of sorted) {\n      let startPos, 
endPos;\n      if (!item.norm.isCollapsed) {\n        startPos = 
item.norm.start;\n        endPos = item.norm.end;\n      } else {\n        
startPos = item.norm.head;\n        if (isWord) {\n          endPos = 
this.doc.getNextWordPos(startPos);\n        } else {\n          endPos = 
this.doc.getNextGraphemePos(startPos);\n        }\n      }\n\n      const res = 
this.doc.replaceRange(startPos, endPos, '');\n      newSelectionsMap[item.idx] =
{\n        anchor: res.newEndPos,\n        head: res.newEndPos\n      };\n    
}\n\n    const afterText = this.doc.getText();\n    this.selections = 
newSelectionsMap;\n    this.desiredCol = 
this.getPrimarySelection().head.col;\n\n    this.undo.recordAction({\n      
beforeText,\n      afterText,\n      beforeSelections,\n      afterSelections: 
this.selections,\n      type: 'delete'\n    });\n\n    this.render();\n    
this._emitCursorChange();\n    this._emitContentChange('delete');\n    
this.scrollToCursor();\n  }\n\n  indent() {\n    const primary = 
this.getPrimarySelection();\n    const norm = 
this.doc.normalizeSelection(primary);\n\n    // Multiline selection block 
indent\n    if (!norm.isCollapsed && (norm.start.line !== norm.end.line || 
(norm.start.col === 0 && norm.end.col === 
this.doc.getLineLength(norm.end.line)))) {\n      const beforeText = 
this.doc.getText();\n      const beforeSelections = 
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));\n\n      const res = 
this.doc.indentBlock(primary);\n      this.selections = [res.selection];\n\n    
const afterText = this.doc.getText();\n      this.undo.recordAction({\n        
beforeText,\n        afterText,\n        beforeSelections,\n        
afterSelections: this.selections,\n        type: 'indent'\n      });\n\n      
this.render();\n      this._emitCursorChange();\n      
this._emitContentChange('indent');\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Single line 
or collapsed cursor: insert 2 spaces\n    this.insertTextAtCarets('  ', 
'indent');\n  }\n\n  outdent() {\n    const primary = 
this.getPrimarySelection();\n    const beforeText = this.doc.getText();\n    
const beforeSelections = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));\n\n    
const res = this.doc.outdentBlock(primary);\n    this.selections = 
[res.selection];\n\n    const afterText = this.doc.getText();\n    
this.undo.recordAction({\n      beforeText,\n      afterText,\n      
beforeSelections,\n      afterSelections: this.selections,\n      type: 
'outdent'\n    });\n\n    this.render();\n    this._emitCursorChange();\n    
this._emitContentChange('outdent');\n  }\n\n  performUndo() {\n    const res = 
this.undo.undo();\n    if (!res) return;\n    this.doc.setText(res.text);\n    
this.selections = res.selections || [{ anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 }, head: { 
line: 0, col: 0 } }];\n    this.render();\n    this._emitCursorChange();\n    
this._emitContentChange('undo');\n    this.scrollToCursor();\n  }\n\n  
performRedo() {\n    const res = this.undo.redo();\n    if (!res) return;\n    
this.doc.setText(res.text);\n    this.selections = res.selections || [{ anchor: 
{ line: 0, col: 0 }, head: { line: 0, col: 0 } }];\n    this.render();\n    
this._emitCursorChange();\n    this._emitContentChange('redo');\n    
this.scrollToCursor();\n  }\n\n  // Virtualized DOM Rendering\n  render() {\n   
const lineCount = this.doc.getLineCount();\n    const totalHeight = lineCount * 
this.lineHeight;\n\n    // Estimate max width\n    let maxLen = 0;\n    for (let
i = 0; i < Math.min(lineCount, 500); i++) {\n      if (this.doc.lines[i].length 
> maxLen) maxLen = this.doc.lines[i].length;\n    }\n    const totalWidth = 
Math.max(800, maxLen * this.charWidth + 100);\n\n    this.sizer.style.height = 
`${totalHeight}px`;\n    this.sizer.style.width = `${totalWidth}px`;\n\n    
const scrollTop = this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;\n    const viewportHeight = 
this.scrollContainer.clientHeight || 600;\n\n    const overscan = 15;\n    const
startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / this.lineHeight) - overscan);\n  
const endLine = Math.min(lineCount - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop + viewportHeight) /
this.lineHeight) + overscan);\n\n    this.layersContainer.style.top = 
`${startLine * this.lineHeight}px`;\n\n    // Render Gutter\n    let gutterHtml 
= '';\n    const activeLine = this.getPrimarySelection().head.line;\n    for 
(let i = startLine; i <= endLine; i++) {\n      const isActive = i === 
activeLine ? ' active' : '';\n      gutterHtml += `<div 
class=\"patchpad-gutter-line${isActive}\" 
style=\"height:${this.lineHeight}px\">${i + 1}</div>`;\n    }\n    
this.gutter.innerHTML = gutterHtml;\n\n    // Render Text Lines\n    let 
linesHtml = '';\n    for (let i = startLine; i <= endLine; i++) {\n      const 
lineText = this.doc.getLine(i);\n      const escaped = 
this._escapeHtml(lineText);\n      linesHtml += `<div class=\"patchpad-line\" 
style=\"height:${this.lineHeight}px\">${escaped || '&nbsp;'}</div>`;\n    }\n   
this.textLayer.innerHTML = linesHtml;\n\n    // Render Selection, Highlights & 
Carets\n    this.renderSelections(startLine, endLine);\n    
this.renderSearchHighlights(startLine, endLine);\n    
this.renderCarets(startLine, endLine);\n  }\n\n  _escapeHtml(str) {\n    if 
(!str) return '';\n    return str\n      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')\n      
.replace(/</g, '&lt;')\n      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')\n      .replace(/\"/g, 
'&quot;')\n      .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');\n  }\n\n  renderSelections(startLine,
endLine) {\n    if (startLine === undefined) {\n      const scrollTop = 
this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;\n      const viewportHeight = 
this.scrollContainer.clientHeight || 600;\n      startLine = Math.max(0, 
Math.floor(scrollTop / this.lineHeight) - 15);\n      endLine = 
Math.min(this.doc.getLineCount() - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop + viewportHeight) / 
this.lineHeight) + 15);\n    }\n\n    let selHtml = '';\n    for (const sel of 
this.selections) {\n      const norm = this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel);\n      
if (norm.isCollapsed) continue;\n\n      const sL = Math.max(startLine, 
norm.start.line);\n      const eL = Math.min(endLine, norm.end.line);\n\n      
for (let L = sL; L <= eL; L++) {\n        const top = (L - startLine) * 
this.lineHeight;\n        let leftCol = 0;\n        let rightCol = 
this.doc.getLineLength(L);\n\n        if (L === norm.start.line) leftCol = 
norm.start.col;\n        if (L === norm.end.line) rightCol = norm.end.col;\n\n  
const leftX = this.colToX(L, leftCol);\n        const rightX = this.colToX(L, 
rightCol);\n        let width = Math.max(4, rightX - leftX);\n\n        if (L < 
norm.end.line && rightCol === this.doc.getLineLength(L)) {\n          width += 
8; // Highlight newline span\n        }\n\n        selHtml += `<div 
class=\"patchpad-selection-rect\" 
style=\"top:${top}px;left:${leftX}px;width:${width}px;height:${this.lineHeight}p
x\"></div>`;\n      }\n    }\n    this.selectionLayer.innerHTML = selHtml;\n  
}\n\n  renderSearchHighlights(startLine, endLine) {\n    if (startLine === 
undefined) {\n      const scrollTop = this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;\n      
const viewportHeight = this.scrollContainer.clientHeight || 600;\n      
startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / this.lineHeight) - 15);\n      
endLine = Math.min(this.doc.getLineCount() - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop + 
viewportHeight) / this.lineHeight) + 15);\n    }\n\n    let hlHtml = '';\n    
for (let i = 0; i < this.searchHighlights.length; i++) {\n      const match = 
this.searchHighlights[i];\n      if (match.end.line < startLine || 
match.start.line > endLine) continue;\n\n      const isActive = i === 
this.activeSearchMatchIndex ? ' active-match' : '';\n      const sL = 
Math.max(startLine, match.start.line);\n      const eL = Math.min(endLine, 
match.end.line);\n\n      for (let L = sL; L <= eL; L++) {\n        const top = 
(L - startLine) * this.lineHeight;\n        let leftCol = (L === 
match.start.line) ? match.start.col : 0;\n        let rightCol = (L === 
match.end.line) ? match.end.col : this.doc.getLineLength(L);\n\n        const 
leftX = this.colToX(L, leftCol);\n        const rightX = this.colToX(L, 
rightCol);\n        const width = Math.max(2, rightX - leftX);\n\n        hlHtml
+= `<div class=\"patchpad-search-rect${isActive}\" 
style=\"top:${top}px;left:${leftX}px;width:${width}px;height:${this.lineHeight}p
x\"></div>`;\n      }\n    }\n    this.searchHighlightsLayer.innerHTML = 
hlHtml;\n  }\n\n  renderCarets(startLine, endLine) {\n    if (startLine === 
undefined) {\n      const scrollTop = this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;\n      
const viewportHeight = this.scrollContainer.clientHeight || 600;\n      
startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / this.lineHeight) - 15);\n      
endLine = Math.min(this.doc.getLineCount() - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop + 
viewportHeight) / this.lineHeight) + 15);\n    }\n\n    let caretHtml = '';\n   
for (const sel of this.selections) {\n      const head = sel.head;\n      if 
(head.line >= startLine && head.line <= endLine) {\n        const top = 
(head.line - startLine) * this.lineHeight;\n        const left = 
this.colToX(head.line, head.col);\n        const blinkClass = this.isFocused ? '
blinking' : '';\n        caretHtml += `<div 
class=\"patchpad-caret${blinkClass}\" 
style=\"top:${top}px;left:${left}px;height:${this.lineHeight}px\"></div>`;\n    
}\n    }\n    this.cursorLayer.innerHTML = caretHtml;\n  }\n}\n\nif (typeof 
module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {\n  module.exports = { 
PatchPadEditorView };\n}\n"}

Reasoning:
**Editor View Design: A Deep Dive**

Alright, let's break down the design for `editor-view.js`. We're going for a 
highly customized DOM-based text editor, so let's meticulously address the 
requirements, prioritizing a seamless, familiar user experience.

First, we need the core structure: A `.patchpad-editor` container, taking 
`tabindex` to grab the input focus, housing an inner layout with a scrollable 
`.editor-scroll-container` wrapping our content. Inside, `.editor-sizer` handles
the virtual dimensions based on line count and max line width, ensuring proper 
scrolling behavior. The `.editor-content` will contain layers for gutter 
(`.editor-gutter`), text lines (`.editor-lines-layer`), selections 
(`.editor-selection-layer`), cursors (`.editor-cursor-layer`), and search 
highlights (`.editor-search-highlights-layer`). Crucially, we'll embed an 
invisible `<textarea>` or `<input>` element *within* this structure. This 
offscreen element will capture all native browser events – keyboard input, 
clipboard interactions, IME support, accessibility – allowing us to leverage 
native browser features while rendering our custom DOM representation.

Next, pixel-perfect character positioning is key. We'll leverage a monospace 
font family and pre-measure `charWidth` and `lineHeight` using a temporary DOM 
element. For accurate column calculation, we'll use `ctx.measureText` on canvas,
even when handling complex Unicode characters, tabs, or accents. Then, 
pixel-to-`line, col` mapping will be calculated by measuring or scanning 
grapheme clusters for subpixel-perfect positioning.

For virtual scrolling, the approach is efficient. We'll calculate the viewport 
with the scroll event, and only render the DOM elements for visible lines, 
maintaining a constant height and updating the selection rectangles and cursors 
accordingly.

We'll render cursors as vertical bars with a blink animation driven by CSS 
keyframes. Mouse selection will handle single-click, double-click word 
selection, and triple-click line selection, alongside multi-caret support with 
Alt/Ctrl/Cmd-click. Dragging will update the selection.

Keyboard events will be handled carefully, with shortcuts for save, find, undo, 
redo, and select all. Tab and Shift+Tab will handle indentation. We'll implement
fine-grained arrow key navigation, supporting word and grapheme movements with 
optional selections. The Backspace, Delete, and Enter keys will handle text 
deletion, line merging, and newline insertion. Printable text will be handled 
within the `beforeinput` or `input` event, for accurate integration with IME and
all other text input methods.

Copy, cut, and paste will use the standard `copy`, `cut`, and `paste` events, 
getting text from selected regions or current lines.

Finally, we'll have auto-scrolling to ensure the primary caret remains visible 
within the viewport at all times.

With this architecture and implementation, we can ensure a custom editor that 
acts and feels as smooth as our favorite document editors!




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/public/js/editor-view.js"
  file_text: 
    // Custom DOM-based Editing Surface for PatchPad
    
    class PatchPadEditorView {
      constructor(containerElement, docModel, undoManager, options = {}) {
        this.container = containerElement;
        this.doc = docModel;
        this.undo = undoManager;
        this.options = options;
    
        // Selections array: each item is { anchor: {line, col}, head: {line, 
col} }
        this.selections = [{
          anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 },
          head: { line: 0, col: 0 }
        }];
    
        this.desiredCol = 0; // For vertical navigation across lines
        this.isDragging = false;
        this.isFocused = false;
        this.searchHighlights = []; // Array of matches
        this.activeSearchMatchIndex = -1;
    
        // Metrics
        this.lineHeight = 24;
        this.charWidth = 8.5; // Monospace approximate, measured dynamically
        this.gutterWidth = 50;
    
        this.onCursorChange = options.onCursorChange || null;
        this.onContentChange = options.onContentChange || null;
        this.onSaveRequest = options.onSaveRequest || null;
        this.onFindRequest = options.onFindRequest || null;
        this.onEscape = options.onEscape || null;
    
        this._initDom();
        this._measureFont();
        this._bindEvents();
        this.render();
      }
    
      _initDom() {
        this.container.innerHTML = '';
        this.container.classList.add('patchpad-editor-wrapper');
    
        // Hidden input capture for keyboard/clipboard events
        this.inputCapture = document.createElement('textarea');
        this.inputCapture.className = 'patchpad-input-capture';
        this.inputCapture.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');
        this.inputCapture.setAttribute('autocomplete', 'off');
        this.inputCapture.setAttribute('autocorrect', 'off');
        this.inputCapture.setAttribute('autocapitalize', 'off');
        this.inputCapture.setAttribute('spellcheck', 'false');
        this.container.appendChild(this.inputCapture);
    
        // Main scroll container
        this.scrollContainer = document.createElement('div');
        this.scrollContainer.className = 'patchpad-scroll-container';
        this.container.appendChild(this.scrollContainer);
    
        // Sizer sets full scroll height & width
        this.sizer = document.createElement('div');
        this.sizer.className = 'patchpad-sizer';
        this.scrollContainer.appendChild(this.sizer);
    
        // Layer container
        this.layersContainer = document.createElement('div');
        this.layersContainer.className = 'patchpad-layers-container';
        this.scrollContainer.appendChild(this.layersContainer);
    
        // Gutter
        this.gutter = document.createElement('div');
        this.gutter.className = 'patchpad-gutter';
        this.layersContainer.appendChild(this.gutter);
    
        // Content container (lines, selections, carets)
        this.contentContainer = document.createElement('div');
        this.contentContainer.className = 'patchpad-content';
        this.layersContainer.appendChild(this.contentContainer);
    
        // Sub-layers inside content
        this.searchHighlightsLayer = document.createElement('div');
        this.searchHighlightsLayer.className = 
'patchpad-search-highlights-layer';
        this.contentContainer.appendChild(this.searchHighlightsLayer);
    
        this.selectionLayer = document.createElement('div');
        this.selectionLayer.className = 'patchpad-selection-layer';
        this.contentContainer.appendChild(this.selectionLayer);
    
        this.textLayer = document.createElement('div');
        this.textLayer.className = 'patchpad-text-layer';
        this.contentContainer.appendChild(this.textLayer);
    
        this.cursorLayer = document.createElement('div');
        this.cursorLayer.className = 'patchpad-cursor-layer';
        this.contentContainer.appendChild(this.cursorLayer);
      }
    
      _measureFont() {
        const testSpan = document.createElement('span');
        testSpan.className = 'patchpad-measure-span';
        testSpan.textContent = 'MMMMMMMMMM1234567890';
        this.textLayer.appendChild(testSpan);
        const rect = testSpan.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (rect.width > 0) {
          this.charWidth = rect.width / 20;
        }
        if (rect.height > 0) {
          this.lineHeight = rect.height;
        }
        testSpan.remove();
    
        // Setup canvas for subpixel text measurement
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        this.canvasCtx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        if (this.canvasCtx) {
          const style = window.getComputedStyle(this.container);
          this.canvasCtx.font = `${style.fontSize || '14px'} ${style.fontFamily 
|| 'monospace'}`;
        }
      }
    
      measureTextWidth(str) {
        if (!str) return 0;
        if (this.canvasCtx) {
          return this.canvasCtx.measureText(str).width;
        }
        return str.length * this.charWidth;
      }
    
      colToX(lineIdx, col) {
        const line = this.doc.getLine(lineIdx);
        const sub = line.substring(0, col);
        return this.measureTextWidth(sub);
      }
    
      xToCol(lineIdx, x) {
        const line = this.doc.getLine(lineIdx);
        if (!line || x <= 0) return 0;
    
        const segs = getGraphemeSegments(line);
        if (segs.length === 0) return 0;
    
        let prevX = 0;
        let prevCol = 0;
    
        for (let i = 0; i < segs.length; i++) {
          const seg = segs[i];
          const col = seg.index + seg.segment.length;
          const curX = this.measureTextWidth(line.substring(0, col));
          const midX = (prevX + curX) / 2;
    
          if (x < midX) {
            return prevCol;
          }
          prevX = curX;
          prevCol = col;
        }
    
        return line.length;
      }
    
      posFromMouseEvent(e) {
        const rect = this.contentContainer.getBoundingClientRect();
        const clientX = e.clientX;
        const clientY = e.clientY;
    
        const relY = clientY - rect.top;
        const relX = clientX - rect.left;
    
        let lineIdx = Math.floor(relY / this.lineHeight);
        lineIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(this.doc.getLineCount() - 1, lineIdx));
    
        const col = this.xToCol(lineIdx, Math.max(0, relX));
        return { line: lineIdx, col };
      }
    
      _bindEvents() {
        // Focus tracking
        this.container.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
          this.focus();
        });
    
        this.inputCapture.addEventListener('focus', () => {
          this.isFocused = true;
          this.container.classList.add('focused');
          this.renderCarets();
        });
    
        this.inputCapture.addEventListener('blur', () => {
          this.isFocused = false;
          this.container.classList.remove('focused');
          this.renderCarets();
        });
    
        // Scrolling
        this.scrollContainer.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
          this.render();
        });
    
        // Mouse Selection & Caret handling
        this.scrollContainer.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
          if (e.button !== 0) return; // Left click only
          this.focus();
    
          const pos = this.posFromMouseEvent(e);
          this.desiredCol = pos.col;
    
          if (e.altKey || ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && !e.shiftKey)) {
            // Add multi-caret
            this.selections.push({
              anchor: { ...pos },
              head: { ...pos }
            });
          } else if (e.shiftKey) {
            // Extend primary selection
            if (this.selections.length === 0) {
              this.selections = [{ anchor: { ...pos }, head: { ...pos } }];
            } else {
              this.selections[this.selections.length - 1].head = { ...pos };
            }
          } else {
            // Single caret
            if (e.detail === 2) {
              // Double click: select word
              const wordRange = this.doc.getWordAtPos(pos);
              this.selections = [{
                anchor: wordRange.start,
                head: wordRange.end
              }];
            } else if (e.detail >= 3) {
              // Triple click: select line
              const lineSel = this.doc.getLineSelection(pos.line);
              this.selections = [lineSel];
            } else {
              this.selections = [{
                anchor: { ...pos },
                head: { ...pos }
              }];
            }
          }
    
          this.isDragging = true;
          this.render();
          this._emitCursorChange();
    
          const onMouseMove = (moveEvent) => {
            if (!this.isDragging) return;
            const curPos = this.posFromMouseEvent(moveEvent);
            this.desiredCol = curPos.col;
            const primary = this.selections[this.selections.length - 1];
            primary.head = { ...curPos };
            this._autoScroll(moveEvent);
            this.render();
            this._emitCursorChange();
          };
    
          const onMouseUp = () => {
            this.isDragging = false;
            window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
            window.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
          };
    
          window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
          window.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
        });
    
        // Keyboard handling
        this.inputCapture.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => 
this._handleKeyDown(e));
        this.inputCapture.addEventListener('beforeinput', (e) => 
this._handleBeforeInput(e));
        this.inputCapture.addEventListener('copy', (e) => this._handleCopy(e));
        this.inputCapture.addEventListener('cut', (e) => this._handleCut(e));
        this.inputCapture.addEventListener('paste', (e) => 
this._handlePaste(e));
      }
    
      _autoScroll(e) {
        const rect = this.scrollContainer.getBoundingClientRect();
        const margin = 20;
        if (e.clientY < rect.top + margin) {
          this.scrollContainer.scrollTop -= 15;
        } else if (e.clientY > rect.bottom - margin) {
          this.scrollContainer.scrollTop += 15;
        }
        if (e.clientX < rect.left + margin) {
          this.scrollContainer.scrollLeft -= 15;
        } else if (e.clientX > rect.right - margin) {
          this.scrollContainer.scrollLeft += 15;
        }
      }
    
      focus() {
        if (document.activeElement !== this.inputCapture) {
          this.inputCapture.focus();
        }
      }
    
      _emitCursorChange() {
        if (this.onCursorChange) {
          const primary = this.getPrimarySelection();
          this.onCursorChange({
            line: primary.head.line + 1,
            col: primary.head.col + 1,
            selectedText: this.doc.getSelectedText(primary),
            caretCount: this.selections.length,
            hasSelection: !this.doc.normalizeSelection(primary).isCollapsed
          });
        }
      }
    
      _emitContentChange(type = 'edit') {
        if (this.onContentChange) {
          this.onContentChange(this.doc.getText(), type);
        }
      }
    
      getPrimarySelection() {
        return this.selections[this.selections.length - 1] || {
          anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 },
          head: { line: 0, col: 0 }
        };
      }
    
      setSelections(sels) {
        this.selections = sels.map(s => ({
          anchor: this.doc.clampPosition(s.anchor),
          head: this.doc.clampPosition(s.head)
        }));
        this.render();
        this._emitCursorChange();
        this.scrollToCursor();
      }
    
      setSearchHighlights(matches, activeIndex = -1) {
        this.searchHighlights = matches || [];
        this.activeSearchMatchIndex = activeIndex;
        this.renderSearchHighlights();
      }
    
      scrollToCursor() {
        const primary = this.getPrimarySelection();
        const top = primary.head.line * this.lineHeight;
        const bottom = top + this.lineHeight;
        const left = this.colToX(primary.head.line, primary.head.col);
        const right = left + 20;
    
        const scrollTop = this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;
        const scrollLeft = this.scrollContainer.scrollLeft;
        const viewHeight = this.scrollContainer.clientHeight;
        const viewWidth = this.scrollContainer.clientWidth;
    
        if (top < scrollTop) {
          this.scrollContainer.scrollTop = top;
        } else if (bottom > scrollTop + viewHeight) {
          this.scrollContainer.scrollTop = bottom - viewHeight;
        }
    
        if (left < scrollLeft) {
          this.scrollContainer.scrollLeft = left;
        } else if (right > scrollLeft + viewWidth) {
          this.scrollContainer.scrollLeft = right - viewWidth;
        }
      }
    
      _handleBeforeInput(e) {
        if (e.inputType === 'insertText' && e.data) {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.insertTextAtCarets(e.data, 'typing');
        }
      }
    
      _handleKeyDown(e) {
        const isMac = navigator.platform.toUpperCase().indexOf('MAC') >= 0;
        const ctrlOrCmd = isMac ? e.metaKey : e.ctrlKey;
    
        // Shortcuts
        if (ctrlOrCmd && !e.altKey && !e.shiftKey) {
          if (e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S') {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (this.onSaveRequest) this.onSaveRequest();
            return;
          }
          if (e.key === 'f' || e.key === 'F') {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (this.onFindRequest) this.onFindRequest();
            return;
          }
          if (e.key === 'z' || e.key === 'Z') {
            e.preventDefault();
            this.performUndo();
            return;
          }
          if (e.key === 'y' || e.key === 'Y') {
            e.preventDefault();
            this.performRedo();
            return;
          }
          if (e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A') {
            e.preventDefault();
            this.selectAll();
            return;
          }
        }
    
        if (ctrlOrCmd && e.shiftKey && (e.key === 'z' || e.key === 'Z')) {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.performRedo();
          return;
        }
    
        // Escape
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (this.onEscape) this.onEscape();
          return;
        }
    
        // Tab / Shift+Tab (Indentation)
        if (e.key === 'Tab') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (e.shiftKey) {
            this.outdent();
          } else {
            this.indent();
          }
          return;
        }
    
        // Enter / Newline
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.insertNewline();
          return;
        }
    
        // Backspace
        if (e.key === 'Backspace') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.deleteBackward(ctrlOrCmd || e.altKey);
          return;
        }
    
        // Delete
        if (e.key === 'Delete') {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.deleteForward(ctrlOrCmd || e.altKey);
          return;
        }
    
        // Navigation Keys
        if (['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'Home', 'End', 
'PageUp', 'PageDown'].includes(e.key)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          this._handleNavigation(e.key, e.shiftKey, ctrlOrCmd || e.altKey, 
ctrlOrCmd);
          return;
        }
    
        // Normal Character Insertion fallback (if beforeinput not fired)
        if (!ctrlOrCmd && !e.altKey && e.key.length === 1) {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.insertTextAtCarets(e.key, 'typing');
        }
      }
    
      _handleNavigation(key, isShift, isWordMod, isDocMod) {
        const isMac = navigator.platform.toUpperCase().indexOf('MAC') >= 0;
    
        this.selections = this.selections.map(sel => {
          let head = { ...sel.head };
          let anchor = isShift ? { ...sel.anchor } : { ...head };
    
          switch (key) {
            case 'ArrowLeft': {
              if (!isShift && !this.doc.isPositionsEqual(sel.anchor, sel.head)) 
{
                // Collapse to start of selection
                const norm = this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel);
                head = { ...norm.start };
                anchor = { ...head };
              } else if (isWordMod) {
                head = this.doc.getPrevWordPos(head);
                if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
              } else {
                head = this.doc.getPrevGraphemePos(head);
                if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
              }
              this.desiredCol = head.col;
              break;
            }
            case 'ArrowRight': {
              if (!isShift && !this.doc.isPositionsEqual(sel.anchor, sel.head)) 
{
                // Collapse to end of selection
                const norm = this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel);
                head = { ...norm.end };
                anchor = { ...head };
              } else if (isWordMod) {
                head = this.doc.getNextWordPos(head);
                if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
              } else {
                head = this.doc.getNextGraphemePos(head);
                if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
              }
              this.desiredCol = head.col;
              break;
            }
            case 'ArrowUp': {
              if (isDocMod) {
                head = { line: 0, col: 0 };
              } else if (head.line > 0) {
                const targetLine = head.line - 1;
                const col = Math.min(this.doc.getLineLength(targetLine), 
this.desiredCol);
                head = { line: targetLine, col };
              } else {
                head = { line: 0, col: 0 };
              }
              if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
              break;
            }
            case 'ArrowDown': {
              if (isDocMod) {
                const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;
                head = { line: lastLine, col: this.doc.getLineLength(lastLine) 
};
              } else if (head.line < this.doc.getLineCount() - 1) {
                const targetLine = head.line + 1;
                const col = Math.min(this.doc.getLineLength(targetLine), 
this.desiredCol);
                head = { line: targetLine, col };
              } else {
                const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;
                head = { line: lastLine, col: this.doc.getLineLength(lastLine) 
};
              }
              if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
              break;
            }
            case 'Home': {
              if (isDocMod) {
                head = { line: 0, col: 0 };
              } else {
                head = { line: head.line, col: 0 };
              }
              this.desiredCol = head.col;
              if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
              break;
            }
            case 'End': {
              if (isDocMod) {
                const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;
                head = { line: lastLine, col: this.doc.getLineLength(lastLine) 
};
              } else {
                head = { line: head.line, col: this.doc.getLineLength(head.line)
};
              }
              this.desiredCol = head.col;
              if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
              break;
            }
            case 'PageUp': {
              const jump = Math.max(1, 
Math.floor(this.scrollContainer.clientHeight / this.lineHeight));
              const targetLine = Math.max(0, head.line - jump);
              head = { line: targetLine, col: 
Math.min(this.doc.getLineLength(targetLine), this.desiredCol) };
              if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
              break;
            }
            case 'PageDown': {
              const jump = Math.max(1, 
Math.floor(this.scrollContainer.clientHeight / this.lineHeight));
              const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;
              const targetLine = Math.min(lastLine, head.line + jump);
              head = { line: targetLine, col: 
Math.min(this.doc.getLineLength(targetLine), this.desiredCol) };
              if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
              break;
            }
          }
    
          return { anchor, head };
        });
    
        this.render();
        this._emitCursorChange();
        this.scrollToCursor();
      }
    
      selectAll() {
        const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;
        this.selections = [{
          anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 },
          head: { line: lastLine, col: this.doc.getLineLength(lastLine) }
        }];
        this.render();
        this._emitCursorChange();
      }
    
      _handleCopy(e) {
        const textToCopy = this.selections.map(s => 
this.doc.getSelectedText(s)).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
        if (textToCopy) {
          e.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', textToCopy);
          e.preventDefault();
        }
      }
    
      _handleCut(e) {
        const textToCopy = this.selections.map(s => 
this.doc.getSelectedText(s)).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
        if (textToCopy) {
          e.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', textToCopy);
          this.deleteBackward(false);
          e.preventDefault();
        }
      }
    
      _handlePaste(e) {
        const pasted = e.clipboardData.getData('text/plain');
        if (pasted) {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.insertTextAtCarets(pasted, 'paste');
        }
      }
    
      insertTextAtCarets(text, type = 'typing') {
        const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
        const beforeSelections = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));
    
        // Sort selections descending by start position to apply edits without 
shifting coordinates
        const sorted = this.selections.map((sel, idx) => ({ sel, idx, norm: 
this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel) }))
          .sort((a, b) => this.doc.comparePositions(b.norm.start, 
a.norm.start));
    
        const newSelectionsMap = [];
    
        for (const item of sorted) {
          const res = this.doc.replaceRange(item.norm.start, item.norm.end, 
text);
          newSelectionsMap[item.idx] = {
            anchor: res.newEndPos,
            head: res.newEndPos
          };
        }
    
        const afterText = this.doc.getText();
        this.selections = newSelectionsMap;
        this.desiredCol = this.getPrimarySelection().head.col;
    
        this.undo.recordAction({
          beforeText,
          afterText,
          beforeSelections,
          afterSelections: this.selections,
          type
        });
    
        this.render();
        this._emitCursorChange();
        this._emitContentChange(type);
        this.scrollToCursor();
      }
    
      insertNewline() {
        this.insertTextAtCarets('\n', 'typing');
      }
    
      deleteBackward(isWord = false) {
        const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
        const beforeSelections = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));
    
        const sorted = this.selections.map((sel, idx) => ({ sel, idx, norm: 
this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel) }))
          .sort((a, b) => this.doc.comparePositions(b.norm.start, 
a.norm.start));
    
        const newSelectionsMap = [];
    
        for (const item of sorted) {
          let startPos, endPos;
          if (!item.norm.isCollapsed) {
            startPos = item.norm.start;
            endPos = item.norm.end;
          } else {
            endPos = item.norm.head;
            if (isWord) {
              startPos = this.doc.getPrevWordPos(endPos);
            } else {
              startPos = this.doc.getPrevGraphemePos(endPos);
            }
          }
    
          const res = this.doc.replaceRange(startPos, endPos, '');
          newSelectionsMap[item.idx] = {
            anchor: res.newEndPos,
            head: res.newEndPos
          };
        }
    
        const afterText = this.doc.getText();
        this.selections = newSelectionsMap;
        this.desiredCol = this.getPrimarySelection().head.col;
    
        this.undo.recordAction({
          beforeText,
          afterText,
          beforeSelections,
          afterSelections: this.selections,
          type: 'backspace'
        });
    
        this.render();
        this._emitCursorChange();
        this._emitContentChange('delete');
        this.scrollToCursor();
      }
    
      deleteForward(isWord = false) {
        const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
        const beforeSelections = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));
    
        const sorted = this.selections.map((sel, idx) => ({ sel, idx, norm: 
this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel) }))
          .sort((a, b) => this.doc.comparePositions(b.norm.start, 
a.norm.start));
    
        const newSelectionsMap = [];
    
        for (const item of sorted) {
          let startPos, endPos;
          if (!item.norm.isCollapsed) {
            startPos = item.norm.start;
            endPos = item.norm.end;
          } else {
            startPos = item.norm.head;
            if (isWord) {
              endPos = this.doc.getNextWordPos(startPos);
            } else {
              endPos = this.doc.getNextGraphemePos(startPos);
            }
          }
    
          const res = this.doc.replaceRange(startPos, endPos, '');
          newSelectionsMap[item.idx] = {
            anchor: res.newEndPos,
            head: res.newEndPos
          };
        }
    
        const afterText = this.doc.getText();
        this.selections = newSelectionsMap;
        this.desiredCol = this.getPrimarySelection().head.col;
    
        this.undo.recordAction({
          beforeText,
          afterText,
          beforeSelections,
          afterSelections: this.selections,
          type: 'delete'
        });
    
        this.render();
        this._emitCursorChange();
        this._emitContentChange('delete');
        this.scrollToCursor();
      }
    
      indent() {
        const primary = this.getPrimarySelection();
        const norm = this.doc.normalizeSelection(primary);
    
        // Multiline selection block indent
        if (!norm.isCollapsed && (norm.start.line !== norm.end.line || 
(norm.start.col === 0 && norm.end.col === 
this.doc.getLineLength(norm.end.line)))) {
          const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
          const beforeSelections = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));
    
          const res = this.doc.indentBlock(primary);
          this.selections = [res.selection];
    
          const afterText = this.doc.getText();
          this.undo.recordAction({
            beforeText,
            afterText,
            beforeSelections,
            afterSelections: this.selections,
            type: 'indent'
          });
    
          this.render();
          this._emitCursorChange();
          this._emitContentChange('indent');
          return;
        }
    
        // Single line or collapsed cursor: insert 2 spaces
        this.insertTextAtCarets('  ', 'indent');
      }
    
      outdent() {
        const primary = this.getPrimarySelection();
        const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
        const beforeSelections = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));
    
        const res = this.doc.outdentBlock(primary);
        this.selections = [res.selection];
    
        const afterText = this.doc.getText();
        this.undo.recordAction({
          beforeText,
          afterText,
          beforeSelections,
          afterSelections: this.selections,
          type: 'outdent'
        });
    
        this.render();
        this._emitCursorChange();
        this._emitContentChange('outdent');
      }
    
      performUndo() {
        const res = this.undo.undo();
        if (!res) return;
        this.doc.setText(res.text);
        this.selections = res.selections || [{ anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 }, 
head: { line: 0, col: 0 } }];
        this.render();
        this._emitCursorChange();
        this._emitContentChange('undo');
        this.scrollToCursor();
      }
    
      performRedo() {
        const res = this.undo.redo();
        if (!res) return;
        this.doc.setText(res.text);
        this.selections = res.selections || [{ anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 }, 
head: { line: 0, col: 0 } }];
        this.render();
        this._emitCursorChange();
        this._emitContentChange('redo');
        this.scrollToCursor();
      }
    
      // Virtualized DOM Rendering
      render() {
        const lineCount = this.doc.getLineCount();
        const totalHeight = lineCount * this.lineHeight;
    
        // Estimate max width
        let maxLen = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(lineCount, 500); i++) {
          if (this.doc.lines[i].length > maxLen) maxLen = 
this.doc.lines[i].length;
        }
        const totalWidth = Math.max(800, maxLen * this.charWidth + 100);
    
        this.sizer.style.height = `${totalHeight}px`;
        this.sizer.style.width = `${totalWidth}px`;
    
        const scrollTop = this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;
        const viewportHeight = this.scrollContainer.clientHeight || 600;
    
        const overscan = 15;
        const startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / this.lineHeight) - 
overscan);
        const endLine = Math.min(lineCount - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop + 
viewportHeight) / this.lineHeight) + overscan);
    
        this.layersContainer.style.top = `${startLine * this.lineHeight}px`;
    
        // Render Gutter
        let gutterHtml = '';
        const activeLine = this.getPrimarySelection().head.line;
        for (let i = startLine; i <= endLine; i++) {
          const isActive = i === activeLine ? ' active' : '';
          gutterHtml += `<div class="patchpad-gutter-line${isActive}" 
style="height:${this.lineHeight}px">${i + 1}</div>`;
        }
        this.gutter.innerHTML = gutterHtml;
    
        // Render Text Lines
        let linesHtml = '';
        for (let i = startLine; i <= endLine; i++) {
          const lineText = this.doc.getLine(i);
          const escaped = this._escapeHtml(lineText);
          linesHtml += `<div class="patchpad-line" 
style="height:${this.lineHeight}px">${escaped || '&nbsp;'}</div>`;
        }
        this.textLayer.innerHTML = linesHtml;
    
        // Render Selection, Highlights & Carets
        this.renderSelections(startLine, endLine);
        this.renderSearchHighlights(startLine, endLine);
        this.renderCarets(startLine, endLine);
      }
    
      _escapeHtml(str) {
        if (!str) return '';
        return str
          .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
          .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
          .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
          .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
          .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
      }
    
      renderSelections(startLine, endLine) {
        if (startLine === undefined) {
          const scrollTop = this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;
          const viewportHeight = this.scrollContainer.clientHeight || 600;
          startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / this.lineHeight) - 15);
          endLine = Math.min(this.doc.getLineCount() - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop +
viewportHeight) / this.lineHeight) + 15);
        }
    
        let selHtml = '';
        for (const sel of this.selections) {
          const norm = this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel);
          if (norm.isCollapsed) continue;
    
          const sL = Math.max(startLine, norm.start.line);
          const eL = Math.min(endLine, norm.end.line);
    
          for (let L = sL; L <= eL; L++) {
            const top = (L - startLine) * this.lineHeight;
            let leftCol = 0;
            let rightCol = this.doc.getLineLength(L);
    
            if (L === norm.start.line) leftCol = norm.start.col;
            if (L === norm.end.line) rightCol = norm.end.col;
    
            const leftX = this.colToX(L, leftCol);
            const rightX = this.colToX(L, rightCol);
            let width = Math.max(4, rightX - leftX);
    
            if (L < norm.end.line && rightCol === this.doc.getLineLength(L)) {
              width += 8; // Highlight newline span
            }
    
            selHtml += `<div class="patchpad-selection-rect" 
style="top:${top}px;left:${leftX}px;width:${width}px;height:${this.lineHeight}px
"></div>`;
          }
        }
        this.selectionLayer.innerHTML = selHtml;
      }
    
      renderSearchHighlights(startLine, endLine) {
        if (startLine === undefined) {
          const scrollTop = this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;
          const viewportHeight = this.scrollContainer.clientHeight || 600;
          startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / this.lineHeight) - 15);
          endLine = Math.min(this.doc.getLineCount() - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop +
viewportHeight) / this.lineHeight) + 15);
        }
    
        let hlHtml = '';
        for (let i = 0; i < this.searchHighlights.length; i++) {
          const match = this.searchHighlights[i];
          if (match.end.line < startLine || match.start.line > endLine) 
continue;
    
          const isActive = i === this.activeSearchMatchIndex ? ' active-match' :
'';
          const sL = Math.max(startLine, match.start.line);
          const eL = Math.min(endLine, match.end.line);
    
          for (let L = sL; L <= eL; L++) {
            const top = (L - startLine) * this.lineHeight;
            let leftCol = (L === match.start.line) ? match.start.col : 0;
            let rightCol = (L === match.end.line) ? match.end.col : 
this.doc.getLineLength(L);
    
            const leftX = this.colToX(L, leftCol);
            const rightX = this.colToX(L, rightCol);
            const width = Math.max(2, rightX - leftX);
    
            hlHtml += `<div class="patchpad-search-rect${isActive}" 
style="top:${top}px;left:${leftX}px;width:${width}px;height:${this.lineHeight}px
"></div>`;
          }
        }
        this.searchHighlightsLayer.innerHTML = hlHtml;
      }
    
      renderCarets(startLine, endLine) {
        if (startLine === undefined) {
          const scrollTop = this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;
          const viewportHeight = this.scrollContainer.clientHeight || 600;
          startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / this.lineHeight) - 15);
          endLine = Math.min(this.doc.getLineCount() - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop +
viewportHeight) / this.lineHeight) + 15);
        }
    
        let caretHtml = '';
        for (const sel of this.selections) {
          const head = sel.head;
          if (head.line >= startLine && head.line <= endLine) {
            const top = (head.line - startLine) * this.lineHeight;
            const left = this.colToX(head.line, head.col);
            const blinkClass = this.isFocused ? ' blinking' : '';
            caretHtml += `<div class="patchpad-caret${blinkClass}" 
style="top:${top}px;left:${left}px;height:${this.lineHeight}px"></div>`;
          }
        }
        this.cursorLayer.innerHTML = caretHtml;
      }
    }
    
    if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
      module.exports = { PatchPadEditorView };
    }

+102|    if (rect.width > 0) {

+109|

+113|    if (this.canvasCtx) {

+116|    }

+118|
+119|  measureTextWidth(str) {
+120|    if (!str) return 0;
+121|    if (this.canvasCtx) {
+122|      return this.canvasCtx.measureText(str).width;
+123|    }

+126|
+127|  colToX(lineIdx, col) {
+128|    const line = this.doc.getLine(lineIdx);
+129|    const sub = line.substring(0, col);
+130|    return this.measureTextWidth(sub);
+131|  }
+132|
+133|  xToCol(lineIdx, x) {
+134|    const line = this.doc.getLine(lineIdx);
+135|    if (!line || x <= 0) return 0;
+136|
+137|    const segs = getGraphemeSegments(line);
+138|    if (segs.length === 0) return 0;
+139|
+140|    let prevX = 0;
+141|    let prevCol = 0;
+142|
+143|    for (let i = 0; i < segs.length; i++) {
+144|      const seg = segs[i];
+145|      const col = seg.index + seg.segment.length;
+146|      const curX = this.measureTextWidth(line.substring(0, col));
+147|      const midX = (prevX + curX) / 2;
+148|
+149|      if (x < midX) {
+150|        return prevCol;
+151|      }
+152|      prevX = curX;
+153|      prevCol = col;
+154|    }
+155|
+156|    return line.length;
+157|  }
+158|
+159|  posFromMouseEvent(e) {

+168|    lineIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(this.doc.getLineCount() - 1, lineIdx));
+169|
+170|    const col = this.xToCol(lineIdx, Math.max(0, relX));
+171|    return { line: lineIdx, col };

+203|      this.desiredCol = pos.col;

+205|      if (e.altKey || ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && !e.shiftKey)) {
+206|        // Add multi-caret

+211|      } else if (e.shiftKey) {

+218|      } else {

+227|        } else if (e.detail >= 3) {

+230|          this.selections = [lineSel];
+231|        } else {

+312|  getPrimarySelection() {

+335|  scrollToCursor() {
+336|    const primary = this.getPrimarySelection();
+337|    const top = primary.head.line * this.lineHeight;
+338|    const bottom = top + this.lineHeight;
+339|    const left = this.colToX(primary.head.line, primary.head.col);
+340|    const right = left + 20;
+341|

+344|    const viewHeight = this.scrollContainer.clientHeight;
+345|    const viewWidth = this.scrollContainer.clientWidth;
+346|
+347|    if (top < scrollTop) {
+348|      this.scrollContainer.scrollTop = top;
+349|    } else if (bottom > scrollTop + viewHeight) {
+350|      this.scrollContainer.scrollTop = bottom - viewHeight;
+351|    }
+352|
+353|    if (left < scrollLeft) {
+354|      this.scrollContainer.scrollLeft = left;
+355|    } else if (right > scrollLeft + viewWidth) {
+356|      this.scrollContainer.scrollLeft = right - viewWidth;
+357|    }
+358|  }

+360|  _handleBeforeInput(e) {
+361|    if (e.inputType === 'insertText' && e.data) {
+362|      e.preventDefault();
