+367|  _handleKeyDown(e) {
+368|    const isMac = navigator.platform.toUpperCase().indexOf('MAC') >= 0;
+369|    const ctrlOrCmd = isMac ? e.metaKey : e.ctrlKey;
+370|
+371|    // Shortcuts
+372|    if (ctrlOrCmd && !e.altKey && !e.shiftKey) {
+373|      if (e.key === 's' || e.key === 'S') {
+374|        e.preventDefault();
+375|        if (this.onSaveRequest) this.onSaveRequest();
+376|        return;
+377|      }
+378|      if (e.key === 'f' || e.key === 'F') {
+379|        e.preventDefault();
+380|        if (this.onFindRequest) this.onFindRequest();
+381|        return;
+382|      }
+383|      if (e.key === 'z' || e.key === 'Z') {
+384|        e.preventDefault();
+385|        this.performUndo();
+386|        return;
+387|      }
+388|      if (e.key === 'y' || e.key === 'Y') {
+389|        e.preventDefault();
+390|        this.performRedo();
+391|        return;
+392|      }
+393|      if (e.key === 'a' || e.key === 'A') {
+394|        e.preventDefault();
+395|        this.selectAll();
+396|        return;
+397|      }
+398|    }
+399|
+400|    if (ctrlOrCmd && e.shiftKey && (e.key === 'z' || e.key === 'Z')) {
+401|      e.preventDefault();
+402|      this.performRedo();
+403|      return;
+404|    }
+405|
+406|    // Escape
+407|    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
+408|      e.preventDefault();
+409|      if (this.onEscape) this.onEscape();
+410|      return;
+411|    }
+412|
+413|    // Tab / Shift+Tab (Indentation)
+414|    if (e.key === 'Tab') {
+415|      e.preventDefault();
+416|      if (e.shiftKey) {
+417|        this.outdent();
+418|      } else {
+419|        this.indent();
+420|      }
+421|      return;
+422|    }
+423|
+424|    // Enter / Newline
+425|    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
+426|      e.preventDefault();
+427|      this.insertNewline();
+428|      return;
+429|    }
+430|
+431|    // Backspace
+432|    if (e.key === 'Backspace') {
+433|      e.preventDefault();
+434|      this.deleteBackward(ctrlOrCmd || e.altKey);
+435|      return;
+436|    }
+437|
+438|    // Delete
+439|    if (e.key === 'Delete') {
+440|      e.preventDefault();
+441|      this.deleteForward(ctrlOrCmd || e.altKey);
+442|      return;
+443|    }
+444|
+445|    // Navigation Keys
+446|    if (['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'Home', 'End', 
'PageUp', 'PageDown'].includes(e.key)) {
+447|      e.preventDefault();
+448|      this._handleNavigation(e.key, e.shiftKey, ctrlOrCmd || e.altKey, 
ctrlOrCmd);
+449|      return;
+450|    }
+451|
+452|    // Normal Character Insertion fallback (if beforeinput not fired)
+453|    if (!ctrlOrCmd && !e.altKey && e.key.length === 1) {
+454|      e.preventDefault();
+455|      this.insertTextAtCarets(e.key, 'typing');
+456|    }
+457|  }
+458|
+459|  _handleNavigation(key, isShift, isWordMod, isDocMod) {
+460|    const isMac = navigator.platform.toUpperCase().indexOf('MAC') >= 0;
+461|
+462|    this.selections = this.selections.map(sel => {
+463|      let head = { ...sel.head };
+464|      let anchor = isShift ? { ...sel.anchor } : { ...head };
+465|
+466|      switch (key) {
+467|        case 'ArrowLeft': {
+468|          if (!isShift && !this.doc.isPositionsEqual(sel.anchor, sel.head))
{
+469|            // Collapse to start of selection
+470|            const norm = this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel);
+471|            head = { ...norm.start };
+472|            anchor = { ...head };
+473|          } else if (isWordMod) {
+474|            head = this.doc.getPrevWordPos(head);
+475|            if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
+476|          } else {
+477|            head = this.doc.getPrevGraphemePos(head);
+478|            if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
+479|          }
+480|          this.desiredCol = head.col;
+481|          break;
+482|        }
+483|        case 'ArrowRight': {
+484|          if (!isShift && !this.doc.isPositionsEqual(sel.anchor, sel.head))
{
+485|            // Collapse to end of selection
+486|            const norm = this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel);
+487|            head = { ...norm.end };
+488|            anchor = { ...head };
+489|          } else if (isWordMod) {
+490|            head = this.doc.getNextWordPos(head);
+491|            if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
+492|          } else {
+493|            head = this.doc.getNextGraphemePos(head);
+494|            if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
+495|          }
+496|          this.desiredCol = head.col;
+497|          break;
+498|        }
+499|        case 'ArrowUp': {
+500|          if (isDocMod) {
+501|            head = { line: 0, col: 0 };
+502|          } else if (head.line > 0) {
+503|            const targetLine = head.line - 1;
+504|            const col = Math.min(this.doc.getLineLength(targetLine), 
this.desiredCol);
+505|            head = { line: targetLine, col };
+506|          } else {
+507|            head = { line: 0, col: 0 };
+508|          }
+509|          if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
+510|          break;
+511|        }
+512|        case 'ArrowDown': {
+513|          if (isDocMod) {
+514|            const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;
+515|            head = { line: lastLine, col: this.doc.getLineLength(lastLine) 
};
+516|          } else if (head.line < this.doc.getLineCount() - 1) {
+517|            const targetLine = head.line + 1;
+518|            const col = Math.min(this.doc.getLineLength(targetLine), 
this.desiredCol);
+519|            head = { line: targetLine, col };
+520|          } else {
+521|            const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;
+522|            head = { line: lastLine, col: this.doc.getLineLength(lastLine) 
};
+523|          }
+524|          if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
+525|          break;
+526|        }
+527|        case 'Home': {
+528|          if (isDocMod) {
+529|            head = { line: 0, col: 0 };
+530|          } else {
+531|            head = { line: head.line, col: 0 };
+532|          }
+533|          this.desiredCol = head.col;
+534|          if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
+535|          break;
+536|        }
+537|        case 'End': {
+538|          if (isDocMod) {
+539|            const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;
+540|            head = { line: lastLine, col: this.doc.getLineLength(lastLine) 
};
+541|          } else {
+542|            head = { line: head.line, col: 
this.doc.getLineLength(head.line) };
+543|          }
+544|          this.desiredCol = head.col;
+545|          if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
+546|          break;
+547|        }
+548|        case 'PageUp': {
+549|          const jump = Math.max(1, 
Math.floor(this.scrollContainer.clientHeight / this.lineHeight));
+550|          const targetLine = Math.max(0, head.line - jump);
+551|          head = { line: targetLine, col: 
Math.min(this.doc.getLineLength(targetLine), this.desiredCol) };
+552|          if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
+553|          break;
+554|        }
+555|        case 'PageDown': {
+556|          const jump = Math.max(1, 
Math.floor(this.scrollContainer.clientHeight / this.lineHeight));
+557|          const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;
+558|          const targetLine = Math.min(lastLine, head.line + jump);
+559|          head = { line: targetLine, col: 
Math.min(this.doc.getLineLength(targetLine), this.desiredCol) };
+560|          if (!isShift) anchor = { ...head };
+561|          break;
+562|        }
+563|      }
+564|
+565|      return { anchor, head };
+566|    });
+567|
+568|    this.render();
+569|    this._emitCursorChange();
+570|    this.scrollToCursor();
+571|  }
+572|
+573|  selectAll() {
+574|    const lastLine = this.doc.getLineCount() - 1;
+575|    this.selections = [{
+576|      anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 },
+577|      head: { line: lastLine, col: this.doc.getLineLength(lastLine) }
+578|    }];
+579|    this.render();
+580|    this._emitCursorChange();
+581|  }
+582|
+583|  _handleCopy(e) {
+584|    const textToCopy = this.selections.map(s => 
this.doc.getSelectedText(s)).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
+585|    if (textToCopy) {
+586|      e.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', textToCopy);
+587|      e.preventDefault();
+588|    }
+589|  }
+590|
+591|  _handleCut(e) {
+592|    const textToCopy = this.selections.map(s => 
this.doc.getSelectedText(s)).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
+593|    if (textToCopy) {
+594|      e.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', textToCopy);
+595|      this.deleteBackward(false);
+596|      e.preventDefault();
+597|    }
+598|  }
+599|
+600|  _handlePaste(e) {
+601|    const pasted = e.clipboardData.getData('text/plain');
+602|    if (pasted) {
+603|      e.preventDefault();
+604|      this.insertTextAtCarets(pasted, 'paste');
+605|    }
+606|  }
+607|
+608|  insertTextAtCarets(text, type = 'typing') {
+609|    const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
+610|    const beforeSelections = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));
+611|
+612|    // Sort selections descending by start position to apply edits without 
shifting coordinates
+613|    const sorted = this.selections.map((sel, idx) => ({ sel, idx, norm: 
this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel) }))
+614|      .sort((a, b) => this.doc.comparePositions(b.norm.start, 
a.norm.start));
+615|
+616|    const newSelectionsMap = [];
+617|
+618|    for (const item of sorted) {
+619|      const res = this.doc.replaceRange(item.norm.start, item.norm.end, 
text);
+620|      newSelectionsMap[item.idx] = {
+621|        anchor: res.newEndPos,
+622|        head: res.newEndPos
+623|      };
+624|    }
+625|
+626|    const afterText = this.doc.getText();
+627|    this.selections = newSelectionsMap;
+628|    this.desiredCol = this.getPrimarySelection().head.col;
+629|
+630|    this.undo.recordAction({
+631|      beforeText,
+632|      afterText,
+633|      beforeSelections,
+634|      afterSelections: this.selections,
+635|      type
+636|    });
+637|
+638|    this.render();
+639|    this._emitCursorChange();
+640|    this._emitContentChange(type);
+641|    this.scrollToCursor();
+642|  }
+643|
+644|  insertNewline() {
+645|    this.insertTextAtCarets('\n', 'typing');
+646|  }
+647|
+648|  deleteBackward(isWord = false) {
+649|    const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
+650|    const beforeSelections = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));
+651|
+652|    const sorted = this.selections.map((sel, idx) => ({ sel, idx, norm: 
this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel) }))
+653|      .sort((a, b) => this.doc.comparePositions(b.norm.start, 
a.norm.start));
+654|
+655|    const newSelectionsMap = [];
+656|
+657|    for (const item of sorted) {
+658|      let startPos, endPos;
+659|      if (!item.norm.isCollapsed) {
+660|        startPos = item.norm.start;
+661|        endPos = item.norm.end;
+662|      } else {
+663|        endPos = item.norm.head;
+664|        if (isWord) {
+665|          startPos = this.doc.getPrevWordPos(endPos);
+666|        } else {
+667|          startPos = this.doc.getPrevGraphemePos(endPos);
+668|        }
+669|      }
+670|
+671|      const res = this.doc.replaceRange(startPos, endPos, '');
+672|      newSelectionsMap[item.idx] = {
+673|        anchor: res.newEndPos,
+674|        head: res.newEndPos
+675|      };
+676|    }
+677|
+678|    const afterText = this.doc.getText();
+679|    this.selections = newSelectionsMap;
+680|    this.desiredCol = this.getPrimarySelection().head.col;
+681|
+682|    this.undo.recordAction({
+683|      beforeText,
+684|      afterText,
+685|      beforeSelections,
+686|      afterSelections: this.selections,
+687|      type: 'backspace'
+688|    });
+689|
+690|    this.render();
+691|    this._emitCursorChange();
+692|    this._emitContentChange('delete');
+693|    this.scrollToCursor();
+694|  }
+695|
+696|  deleteForward(isWord = false) {
+697|    const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
+698|    const beforeSelections = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));
+699|
+700|    const sorted = this.selections.map((sel, idx) => ({ sel, idx, norm: 
this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel) }))
+701|      .sort((a, b) => this.doc.comparePositions(b.norm.start, 
a.norm.start));
+702|
+703|    const newSelectionsMap = [];
+704|
+705|    for (const item of sorted) {
+706|      let startPos, endPos;
+707|      if (!item.norm.isCollapsed) {
+708|        startPos = item.norm.start;
+709|        endPos = item.norm.end;
+710|      } else {
+711|        startPos = item.norm.head;
+712|        if (isWord) {
+713|          endPos = this.doc.getNextWordPos(startPos);
+714|        } else {
+715|          endPos = this.doc.getNextGraphemePos(startPos);
+716|        }
+717|      }
+718|
+719|      const res = this.doc.replaceRange(startPos, endPos, '');
+720|      newSelectionsMap[item.idx] = {
+721|        anchor: res.newEndPos,
+722|        head: res.newEndPos
+723|      };
+724|    }
+725|
+726|    const afterText = this.doc.getText();
+727|    this.selections = newSelectionsMap;
+728|    this.desiredCol = this.getPrimarySelection().head.col;
+729|
+730|    this.undo.recordAction({
+731|      beforeText,
+732|      afterText,
+733|      beforeSelections,
+734|      afterSelections: this.selections,
+735|      type: 'delete'
+736|    });
+737|
+738|    this.render();
+739|    this._emitCursorChange();
+740|    this._emitContentChange('delete');
+741|    this.scrollToCursor();
+742|  }
+743|
+744|  indent() {
+745|    const primary = this.getPrimarySelection();
+746|    const norm = this.doc.normalizeSelection(primary);
+747|
+748|    // Multiline selection block indent
+749|    if (!norm.isCollapsed && (norm.start.line !== norm.end.line || 
(norm.start.col === 0 && norm.end.col === 
this.doc.getLineLength(norm.end.line)))) {
+750|      const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
+751|      const beforeSelections = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));
+752|
+753|      const res = this.doc.indentBlock(primary);
+754|      this.selections = [res.selection];
+755|
+756|      const afterText = this.doc.getText();
+757|      this.undo.recordAction({
+758|        beforeText,
+759|        afterText,
+760|        beforeSelections,
+761|        afterSelections: this.selections,
+762|        type: 'indent'
+763|      });
+764|
+765|      this.render();
+766|      this._emitCursorChange();
+767|      this._emitContentChange('indent');
+768|      return;
+769|    }
+770|
+771|    // Single line or collapsed cursor: insert 2 spaces
+772|    this.insertTextAtCarets('  ', 'indent');
+773|  }
+774|
+775|  outdent() {
+776|    const primary = this.getPrimarySelection();
+777|    const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
+778|    const beforeSelections = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.selections));
+779|
+780|    const res = this.doc.outdentBlock(primary);
+781|    this.selections = [res.selection];
+782|
+783|    const afterText = this.doc.getText();
+784|    this.undo.recordAction({
+785|      beforeText,
+786|      afterText,
+787|      beforeSelections,
+788|      afterSelections: this.selections,
+789|      type: 'outdent'
+790|    });
+791|
+792|    this.render();
+793|    this._emitCursorChange();
+794|    this._emitContentChange('outdent');
+795|  }
+796|
+797|  performUndo() {
+798|    const res = this.undo.undo();
+799|    if (!res) return;
+800|    this.doc.setText(res.text);
+801|    this.selections = res.selections || [{ anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 }, 
head: { line: 0, col: 0 } }];
+802|    this.render();
+803|    this._emitCursorChange();
+804|    this._emitContentChange('undo');
+805|    this.scrollToCursor();
+806|  }
+807|
+808|  performRedo() {
+809|    const res = this.undo.redo();
+810|    if (!res) return;
+811|    this.doc.setText(res.text);
+812|    this.selections = res.selections || [{ anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 }, 
head: { line: 0, col: 0 } }];
+813|    this.render();
+814|    this._emitCursorChange();
+815|    this._emitContentChange('redo');
+816|    this.scrollToCursor();
+817|  }
+818|
+819|  // Virtualized DOM Rendering
+820|  render() {
+821|    const lineCount = this.doc.getLineCount();
+822|    const totalHeight = lineCount * this.lineHeight;
+823|
+824|    // Estimate max width
+825|    let maxLen = 0;
+826|    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(lineCount, 500); i++) {
+827|      if (this.doc.lines[i].length > maxLen) maxLen = 
this.doc.lines[i].length;
+828|    }
+829|    const totalWidth = Math.max(800, maxLen * this.charWidth + 100);
+830|
+831|    this.sizer.style.height = `${totalHeight}px`;
+832|    this.sizer.style.width = `${totalWidth}px`;
+833|
+834|    const scrollTop = this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;
+835|    const viewportHeight = this.scrollContainer.clientHeight || 600;
+836|
+837|    const overscan = 15;
+838|    const startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / this.lineHeight) -
overscan);
+839|    const endLine = Math.min(lineCount - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop + 
viewportHeight) / this.lineHeight) + overscan);
+840|
+841|    this.layersContainer.style.top = `${startLine * this.lineHeight}px`;
+842|
+843|    // Render Gutter
+844|    let gutterHtml = '';
+845|    const activeLine = this.getPrimarySelection().head.line;
+846|    for (let i = startLine; i <= endLine; i++) {
+847|      const isActive = i === activeLine ? ' active' : '';
+848|      gutterHtml += `<div class="patchpad-gutter-line${isActive}" 
style="height:${this.lineHeight}px">${i + 1}</div>`;
+849|    }
+850|    this.gutter.innerHTML = gutterHtml;
+851|
+852|    // Render Text Lines
+853|    let linesHtml = '';
+854|    for (let i = startLine; i <= endLine; i++) {
+855|      const lineText = this.doc.getLine(i);
+856|      const escaped = this._escapeHtml(lineText);
+857|      linesHtml += `<div class="patchpad-line" 
style="height:${this.lineHeight}px">${escaped || '&nbsp;'}</div>`;
+858|    }
+859|    this.textLayer.innerHTML = linesHtml;
+860|
+861|    // Render Selection, Highlights & Carets
+862|    this.renderSelections(startLine, endLine);
+863|    this.renderSearchHighlights(startLine, endLine);
+864|    this.renderCarets(startLine, endLine);
+865|  }
+866|
+867|  _escapeHtml(str) {
+868|    if (!str) return '';
+869|    return str
+870|      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
+871|      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
+872|      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
+873|      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
+874|      .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
+875|  }
+876|
+877|  renderSelections(startLine, endLine) {
+878|    if (startLine === undefined) {
+879|      const scrollTop = this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;
+880|      const viewportHeight = this.scrollContainer.clientHeight || 600;
+881|      startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / this.lineHeight) - 
15);
+882|      endLine = Math.min(this.doc.getLineCount() - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop 
+ viewportHeight) / this.lineHeight) + 15);
+883|    }
+884|
+885|    let selHtml = '';
+886|    for (const sel of this.selections) {
+887|      const norm = this.doc.normalizeSelection(sel);
+888|      if (norm.isCollapsed) continue;
+889|
+890|      const sL = Math.max(startLine, norm.start.line);
+891|      const eL = Math.min(endLine, norm.end.line);
+892|
+893|      for (let L = sL; L <= eL; L++) {
+894|        const top = (L - startLine) * this.lineHeight;
+895|        let leftCol = 0;
+896|        let rightCol = this.doc.getLineLength(L);
+897|
+898|        if (L === norm.start.line) leftCol = norm.start.col;
+899|        if (L === norm.end.line) rightCol = norm.end.col;
+900|
+901|        const leftX = this.colToX(L, leftCol);
+902|        const rightX = this.colToX(L, rightCol);
+903|        let width = Math.max(4, rightX - leftX);
+904|
+905|        if (L < norm.end.line && rightCol === this.doc.getLineLength(L)) {
+906|          width += 8; // Highlight newline span
+907|        }
+908|
+909|        selHtml += `<div class="patchpad-selection-rect" 
style="top:${top}px;left:${leftX}px;width:${width}px;height:${this.lineHeight}px
"></div>`;
+910|      }
+911|    }
+912|    this.selectionLayer.innerHTML = selHtml;
+913|  }
+914|
+915|  renderSearchHighlights(startLine, endLine) {
+916|    if (startLine === undefined) {
+917|      const scrollTop = this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;
+918|      const viewportHeight = this.scrollContainer.clientHeight || 600;
+919|      startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / this.lineHeight) - 
15);
+920|      endLine = Math.min(this.doc.getLineCount() - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop 
+ viewportHeight) / this.lineHeight) + 15);
+921|    }
+922|
+923|    let hlHtml = '';
+924|    for (let i = 0; i < this.searchHighlights.length; i++) {
+925|      const match = this.searchHighlights[i];
+926|      if (match.end.line < startLine || match.start.line > endLine) 
continue;
+927|
+928|      const isActive = i === this.activeSearchMatchIndex ? ' active-match' 
: '';
+929|      const sL = Math.max(startLine, match.start.line);
+930|      const eL = Math.min(endLine, match.end.line);
+931|
+932|      for (let L = sL; L <= eL; L++) {
+933|        const top = (L - startLine) * this.lineHeight;
+934|        let leftCol = (L === match.start.line) ? match.start.col : 0;
+935|        let rightCol = (L === match.end.line) ? match.end.col : 
this.doc.getLineLength(L);
+936|
+937|        const leftX = this.colToX(L, leftCol);
+938|        const rightX = this.colToX(L, rightCol);
+939|        const width = Math.max(2, rightX - leftX);
+940|
+941|        hlHtml += `<div class="patchpad-search-rect${isActive}" 
style="top:${top}px;left:${leftX}px;width:${width}px;height:${this.lineHeight}px
"></div>`;
+942|      }
+943|    }
+944|    this.searchHighlightsLayer.innerHTML = hlHtml;
+945|  }
+946|
+947|  renderCarets(startLine, endLine) {
+948|    if (startLine === undefined) {
+949|      const scrollTop = this.scrollContainer.scrollTop;
+950|      const viewportHeight = this.scrollContainer.clientHeight || 600;
+951|      startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / this.lineHeight) - 
15);
+952|      endLine = Math.min(this.doc.getLineCount() - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop 
+ viewportHeight) / this.lineHeight) + 15);
+953|    }
+954|
+955|    let caretHtml = '';
+956|    for (const sel of this.selections) {
+957|      const head = sel.head;
+958|      if (head.line >= startLine && head.line <= endLine) {
+959|        const top = (head.line - startLine) * this.lineHeight;
+960|        const left = this.colToX(head.line, head.col);
+961|        const blinkClass = this.isFocused ? ' blinking' : '';

+963|      }
+964|    }

Reasoning:
**Architecting the PatchPad Application**

Okay, let's start by mapping out the application's architecture. I need to 
design the core components: `api.js`, `app.js`, `index.html`, and `style.css`. 
Before diving into code, let's meticulously review every requirement outlined in
`overview.md`, `editing.md`, `persistence.md`, `conflict-safety.md`, and 
`interface.md`. I need to ensure nothing slips through the cracks. This is a 
crucial step to avoid reworking later on.

First up, document identity. The report is called "incident-alpha," and the 
title should be "Northwind API Incident Report." The author is "Riley Stone". I 
need to ensure the seed content and metadata remain intact and are only created 
once on application startup. Each save will create a new revision, starting at 
revision 1. The report title must always be visible in the editor's interface 
for easy identification. We also need a server-backed list of available reports,
including their IDs, titles, and authors. This will be the foundation.

Now for saving and persistence. The interface must clearly indicate whether the 
report is saved or has unsaved changes. Saves should create a new SQLite 
revision only when the content has changed; otherwise, it should not. Reloading 
must revert to the latest saved content, discarding any unsaved work. We also 
need a revision history with numbers and timestamps. Previewing old revisions 
must be possible without altering the saved history. Restoring a revision should
open it as an unsaved action.

Conflict safety is paramount. Saves are based on the revision the browser 
started editing, and if another tab has saved a newer version, I need to reject 
the stale save and keep the newer version on the server. The rejected tab's 
unsaved draft must remain in the editor with a conflict message. The user should
be able to discard that draft. The server needs to enforce this save safety, 
rejecting stale, malformed, or contradictory requests with a 409 status code for
stale saves and a non-success code for other invalid requests.

We are not using any ready-made editor; I'll be building the editing surface 
myself with DOM, canvas, or SVG. It should show line numbers and the current 
cursor position. Word shortcuts using Ctrl/Alt + Left/Right are needed, along 
with multiple carets using Alt/Ctrl+Click. Tab/Shift+Tab for indentation, and 
copy/cut/paste, including multiline and tabbed text, should be fully supported. 
Undo/Redo is also required, and all single actions like "Replace All" should 
behave as single undo/redo actions.

Find and replace needs to function. I'll need to provide find and replace 
functionality, including moving through matches, replacing one, and replacing 
all matches. The find results must be reachable in both directions without 
returning to the mouse. Escape key behaviour should focus the Find input or 
return to the editor.

The interface must be designed to be intuitive and easy to use. Controls like 
"Save," "Undo," "Redo," "Find Next," "Replace Current," "Replace All," and the 
revision history must be easily discoverable. I need readable typography, good 
contrast, consistent spacing, and a clear visual hierarchy. It needs to work 
well at a desktop size (1280x800).

Finally, the application manifest is important. I'll make sure there is an 
`APP_MANIFEST.md` file beside `/app/package.json`. The start command will be 
`npm start`, and the SQLite path must be `/app/patchpad.db`.

Now, let's start with designing `api.js`. This module will encapsulate all fetch
calls to the server:

*   `fetchReports()`: `GET /api/reports`
*   `fetchReport(id)`: `GET /api/reports/:id`
*   `saveReport(id, baseRevision, content, comment)`: `POST 
/api/reports/:id/save` with a body of `{ baseRevision, content, documentId: id, 
comment }`
*   `fetchRevisions(id)`: `GET /api/reports/:id/revisions`
*   `fetchRevisionContent(id, revNum)`: `GET /api/reports/:id/revisions/:revNum`

I'll write this file at `/app/public/js/api.js`.

    // API client for PatchPad backend
    
    const Api = {
      async listReports() {
        const res = await fetch('/api/reports');
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to list reports: 
${res.statusText}`);
        return await res.json();
      },
    
      async getReport(id) {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`);
        if (!res.ok) {
          const errData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
          const err = new Error(errData.error || `Failed to fetch report: 
${res.statusText}`);
          err.status = res.status;
          throw err;
        }
        return await res.json();
      },
    
      async saveReport(id, baseRevision, content, comment = '') {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/save`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            documentId: id,
            baseRevision,
            content,
            comment
          })
        });
    
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    
        if (!res.ok) {
          const err = new Error(data.error || `Save failed: ${res.statusText}`);
          err.status = res.status;
          err.serverRevision = data.serverRevision;
          throw err;
        }
    
        return data;
      },
    
      async listRevisions(id) {
        const res = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/revisions`);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to list revisions: 
${res.statusText}`);
        return await res.json();
      },
    
      async getRevisionContent(id, revNum) {
        const res = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/revisions/${revNum}`);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to fetch revision: 
${res.statusText}`);
        return await res.json();
      }
    };
    
    if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
      module.exports = { Api };
    }

findEngine.updateMatches(docModel);\n\n    if (!query) {\n

docModel);\n    const current = findEngine.getCurrentMatch();\n\n

${matches.length}`;\n    editorView.setSearchHighlights(matches, 
findEngine.currentIndex);\n  }\n\n  function jumpToCurrentMatch() {\n    const 
match = findEngine.getCurrentMatch();\n    if (!match) return;\n    
el.matchCount.textContent = `${findEngine.currentIndex + 1} of 
${findEngine.matches.length}`;\n    editorView.setSelections([{ anchor: 
match.start, head: match.end }]);\n    
editorView.setSearchHighlights(findEngine.matches, findEngine.currentIndex);\n  
editorView.scrollToCursor();\n  }\n\n  // Find Event Listeners\n

});\n\n  el.btnFindNext.addEventListener('click', () => {\n    
findEngine.nextMatch();\n    jumpToCurrentMatch();\n  });\n\n  
el.btnFindPrev.addEventListener('click', () => {\n    findEngine.prevMatch();\n 
jumpToCurrentMatch();\n  });\n\n  function replaceCurrentMatch() {\n    
findEngine.setReplaceText(el.replaceInput.value);\n    const res = 
findEngine.replaceCurrent(docModel);\n    if (!res) return;\n\n

res.afterText,\n      beforeSelections: [res.selection],\n      afterSelections:
[res.selection],\n      type: 'replace',\n      forceNewGroup: true\n    });\n\n

showToast('No matches to replace', 'info');\n      return;\n    }\n\n

res.afterText,\n      beforeSelections: 
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(editorView.selections)),\n      afterSelections: 
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(editorView.selections)),\n      type: 'replace_all',\n
forceNewGroup: true\n    });\n\n    updateDirtyState();\n    
updateDocumentStats();\n    updateFindMatches();\n    editorView.render();\n    
showToast(`Replaced ${res.count} occurrence(s)`, 'success');\n  }\n\n  
el.btnReplaceCurrent.addEventListener('click', replaceCurrentMatch);\n  
el.btnReplaceAll.addEventListener('click', replaceAllMatches);\n\n  // Toolbar &
General Action Listeners\n  el.btnSave.addEventListener('click', () =>

Reasoning:
**Designing the App Orchestrator (`app.js`)**

Okay, let's break down what `app.js` needs to do. My thinking is, first and 
foremost, state management. I need a central place to hold all the crucial bits:
the active document ID (`currentDocId`), its metadata (`docMetadata`), the base 
revision the user started with (`baseRevision`), the last saved content 
(`lastSavedContent`), and of course, flags for saving state (`isSaving`), and 
whether the content is dirty (`isDirty`). I'll use a compute property for 
`isDirty` based on `docModel.getText() !== lastSavedContent`. Crucially, I'll 
need instances of the `FindReplaceEngine`, `UndoManager`, `DocumentModel`, and 
`PatchPadEditorView`.

Then comes conflict handling. This is critical. When the save returns a 409 
(conflict), I *must* keep the user's unsaved work untouched in the `docModel`. 
I'll present a modal or banner. It will display a clear message about the 
conflict and offer the user options: "Keep Local Draft," "Compare with Server 
Revision," and "Reload from Server."  The "Reload" option will, of course, 
necessitate a confirmation step to avoid accidental data loss.

Revision history and previewing will be another key element. A "Revision 
History" button will toggle a sidebar. The sidebar will list revisions with 
their numbers, timestamps, character counts, and preview/restore actions.  The 
preview itself will be a modal with a read-only view of the revision's content. 
A "Restore" button there will load the revision into the editor as an unsaved 
change and recorded through the `undoManager`.

The find & replace functionality needs a well-designed panel.  A toggle button 
or `Ctrl+F` will show/hide the panel. Inputs for search and replace, a case 
sensitivity option, and match count indicators will be essential. Navigating 
matches (prev/next) and replacing functionality need clear keyboard shortcuts 
and UI controls. I must ensure proper focus management, from the find input to 
the editor and back again. The search will update in real-time. Replacing 
actions will be recorded in the `UndoManager`.

Next up, the report/document switcher (or a similar construct) is required. I'll
include a dropdown or menu in the header with a list of saved reports with 
essential data. Switching will require confirming if there are unsaved changes 
before loading the new document.

Then there's user feedback. I'll need unobtrusive toast notifications for save 
confirmations, no-change states, replace counts, revision restores, and 
crucially, error messages.

Finally, a status bar to provide live information. I'll show line and column, 
total document statistics, the current base revision, and an indication of the 
save status, including an unsaved/saved/saving/conflict state.
Now I can start to put it all together.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/public/js/app.js"
  file_text: 
    // Main Application Controller for PatchPad
    
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
      const state = {
        currentDocId: 'incident-alpha',
        docMetadata: null,
        baseRevision: 1,
        lastSavedContent: '',
        reportsList: [],
        isDirty: false,
        previewRevision: null
      };
    
      // UI Elements
      const el = {
        // Header & Document Info
        docTitle: document.getElementById('doc-title'),
        docAuthor: document.getElementById('doc-author'),
        reportSelect: document.getElementById('report-select'),
        
        // Toolbar Buttons
        btnSave: document.getElementById('btn-save'),
        btnUndo: document.getElementById('btn-undo'),
        btnRedo: document.getElementById('btn-redo'),
        btnFind: document.getElementById('btn-find'),
        btnHistory: document.getElementById('btn-history'),
        saveBadge: document.getElementById('save-badge'),
        
        // Find & Replace Panel
        findPanel: document.getElementById('find-panel'),
        findInput: document.getElementById('find-input'),
        replaceInput: document.getElementById('replace-input'),
        matchCount: document.getElementById('match-count'),
        btnFindPrev: document.getElementById('btn-find-prev'),
        btnFindNext: document.getElementById('btn-find-next'),
        btnReplaceCurrent: document.getElementById('btn-replace-current'),
        btnReplaceAll: document.getElementById('btn-replace-all'),
        btnCloseFind: document.getElementById('btn-close-find'),
        caseSensitiveCheckbox: document.getElementById('find-case-sensitive'),
        
        // Editor Container
        editorContainer: document.getElementById('editor-container'),
        
        // Revision History Sidebar
        historySidebar: document.getElementById('history-sidebar'),
        historyList: document.getElementById('history-list'),
        btnCloseHistory: document.getElementById('btn-close-history'),
        
        // Status Bar
        statusCursor: document.getElementById('status-cursor'),
        statusStats: document.getElementById('status-stats'),
        statusRevision: document.getElementById('status-revision'),
        statusSaveState: document.getElementById('status-save-state'),
        
        // Modals
        previewModal: document.getElementById('preview-modal'),
        previewTitle: document.getElementById('preview-title'),
        previewMeta: document.getElementById('preview-meta'),
        previewContent: document.getElementById('preview-content'),
        btnRestorePreview: document.getElementById('btn-restore-preview'),
        btnClosePreview: document.getElementById('btn-close-preview'),
        
        conflictModal: document.getElementById('conflict-modal'),
        conflictMessage: document.getElementById('conflict-message'),
        btnConflictKeep: document.getElementById('btn-conflict-keep'),
        btnConflictPreview: document.getElementById('btn-conflict-preview'),
        btnConflictReload: document.getElementById('btn-conflict-reload'),
    
        // Toast Container
        toastContainer: document.getElementById('toast-container')
      };
    
      // Toast notification helper
      function showToast(message, type = 'info', duration = 3500) {
        const toast = document.createElement('div');
        toast.className = `patchpad-toast toast-${type}`;
        toast.innerHTML = `<span class="toast-text">${message}</span>`;
        el.toastContainer.appendChild(toast);
        setTimeout(() => {
          toast.classList.add('show');
        }, 10);
        setTimeout(() => {
          toast.classList.remove('show');
          setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 300);
        }, duration);
      }
    
      // Initialize Core Models
      const docModel = new DocumentModel('');
      const undoManager = new UndoManager();
      const findEngine = new FindReplaceEngine();
    
      // Initialize Custom Editor View
      const editorView = new PatchPadEditorView(el.editorContainer, docModel, 
undoManager, {
        onCursorChange: (info) => {
          if (info.caretCount > 1) {
            el.statusCursor.textContent = `${info.caretCount} carets (Primary Ln
${info.line}, Col ${info.col})`;
          } else if (info.hasSelection) {
            const charCount = info.selectedText.length;
            el.statusCursor.textContent = `Ln ${info.line}, Col ${info.col} 
(${charCount} selected)`;
          } else {
            el.statusCursor.textContent = `Ln ${info.line}, Col ${info.col}`;
          }
        },
        onContentChange: (text, type) => {
          updateDirtyState();
          updateDocumentStats();
          if (findEngine.query) {
            updateFindMatches();
          }
        },
        onSaveRequest: () => {
          saveCurrentReport();
        },
        onFindRequest: () => {
          toggleFindPanel(true);
        },
        onEscape: () => {
          // Escape from editing area focuses Find input
          if (!el.findPanel.classList.contains('hidden')) {
            el.findInput.focus();
            el.findInput.select();
          } else {
            toggleFindPanel(true);
          }
        }
      });
    
      // Undo/Redo button status listener
      undoManager.setOnChange(({ canUndo, canRedo }) => {
        el.btnUndo.disabled = !canUndo;
        el.btnRedo.disabled = !canRedo;
      });
    
      function updateDocumentStats() {
        const lineCount = docModel.getLineCount();
        const charCount = docModel.getText().length;
        el.statusStats.textContent = `${lineCount.toLocaleString()} lines, 
${charCount.toLocaleString()} characters`;
      }
    
      function updateDirtyState() {
        const currentText = docModel.getText();
        const isDirty = currentText !== state.lastSavedContent;
        state.isDirty = isDirty;
    
        if (isDirty) {
          el.saveBadge.textContent = 'Unsaved changes';
          el.saveBadge.className = 'status-badge badge-unsaved';
          el.statusSaveState.textContent = '● Unsaved changes';
          el.statusSaveState.className = 'status-unsaved';
        } else {
          el.saveBadge.textContent = 'Saved';
          el.saveBadge.className = 'status-badge badge-saved';
          el.statusSaveState.textContent = '✓ Saved';
          el.statusSaveState.className = 'status-saved';
        }
      }
    
      // Load Document from Server
      async function loadDocument(id) {
        try {
          const doc = await Api.getReport(id);
          state.currentDocId = doc.id;
          state.docMetadata = doc;
          state.baseRevision = doc.current_revision;
          state.lastSavedContent = doc.content;
    
          // Update Header
          el.docTitle.textContent = doc.title;
          el.docAuthor.textContent = doc.author ? `Author: ${doc.author}` : '';
          el.statusRevision.textContent = `Revision ${doc.current_revision}`;
    
          // Update Editor
          docModel.setText(doc.content);
          undoManager.clear();
          editorView.setSelections([{ anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 }, head: { line:
0, col: 0 } }]);
          editorView.render();
    
          updateDirtyState();
          updateDocumentStats();
          refreshRevisionsList();
    
          showToast(`Loaded "${doc.title}" (Rev ${doc.current_revision})`, 
'info', 2000);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Failed to load report:', err);
          showToast(`Error loading report: ${err.message}`, 'error', 5000);
        }
      }
    
      // Load Reports List
      async function loadReportsList() {
        try {
          const reports = await Api.listReports();
          state.reportsList = reports;
          el.reportSelect.innerHTML = '';
          for (const r of reports) {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = r.id;
            opt.textContent = `${r.title} (${r.author || 'Unknown'})`;
            if (r.id === state.currentDocId) {
              opt.selected = true;
            }
            el.reportSelect.appendChild(opt);
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Failed to load reports list:', err);
        }
      }
    
      // Save Report
      async function saveCurrentReport() {
        const currentText = docModel.getText();
    
        if (!state.isDirty) {
          showToast('Document is already up to date (no changes to save)', 
'info', 2500);
          return;
        }
    
        el.btnSave.disabled = true;
        el.saveBadge.textContent = 'Saving...';
        el.saveBadge.className = 'status-badge badge-saving';
        el.statusSaveState.textContent = '⟳ Saving...';
    
        try {
          const res = await Api.saveReport(
            state.currentDocId,
            state.baseRevision,
            currentText,
            'Saved from PatchPad editor'
          );
    
          if (res.saved) {
            state.baseRevision = res.revision;
            state.lastSavedContent = currentText;
            el.statusRevision.textContent = `Revision ${res.revision}`;
            updateDirtyState();
            refreshRevisionsList();
            showToast(`Report saved successfully as Revision ${res.revision}`, 
'success');
          } else {
            updateDirtyState();
            showToast('Document unchanged', 'info');
          }
        } catch (err) {
          if (err.status === 409) {
            // Save conflict
            el.saveBadge.textContent = 'Conflict';
            el.saveBadge.className = 'status-badge badge-conflict';
            el.statusSaveState.textContent = '⚠ Conflict';
            el.statusSaveState.className = 'status-conflict';
    
            el.conflictMessage.innerHTML = `
              Another user or browser tab saved a newer version of this report 
(<strong>Revision ${err.serverRevision || 'newer'}</strong>).<br><br>
              Your current unsaved edits have been preserved in the editor. 
Choose an action below:
            `;
            el.conflictModal.classList.remove('hidden');
          } else {
            showToast(`Save failed: ${err.message}`, 'error', 5000);
            updateDirtyState();
          }
        } finally {
          el.btnSave.disabled = false;
        }
      }
    
      // Revision History
      async function refreshRevisionsList() {
        try {
          const revs = await Api.listRevisions(state.currentDocId);
          el.historyList.innerHTML = '';
    
          for (const r of revs) {
            const item = document.createElement('div');
            item.className = 'history-item';
            if (r.revision_number === state.baseRevision) {
              item.classList.add('current-revision');
            }
    
            const dateStr = new Date(r.created_at).toLocaleString();
            item.innerHTML = `
              <div class="history-item-header">
                <span class="history-rev-badge">Rev ${r.revision_number}</span>
                ${r.revision_number === state.baseRevision ? '<span 
class="history-current-tag">Current Base</span>' : ''}
              </div>
              <div class="history-item-meta">
                <span class="history-date">${dateStr}</span>
                <span class="history-chars">${(r.char_count || 
0).toLocaleString()} chars</span>
              </div>
              ${r.comment ? `<div class="history-comment">${r.comment}</div>` : 
''}
              <div class="history-item-actions">
                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-secondary btn-preview-rev" 
data-rev="${r.revision_number}">Preview</button>
                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary btn-restore-rev" 
data-rev="${r.revision_number}">Restore</button>
              </div>
            `;
            el.historyList.appendChild(item);
          }
    
          // Bind Preview and Restore buttons
          el.historyList.querySelectorAll('.btn-preview-rev').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
              const revNum = parseInt(btn.dataset.rev, 10);
              previewRevision(revNum);
            });
          });
    
          el.historyList.querySelectorAll('.btn-restore-rev').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
              const revNum = parseInt(btn.dataset.rev, 10);
              restoreRevision(revNum);
            });
          });
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Failed to load revisions:', err);
        }
      }
    
      async function previewRevision(revNum) {
        try {
          const rev = await Api.getRevisionContent(state.currentDocId, revNum);
          state.previewRevision = rev;
    
          el.previewTitle.textContent = `Preview: ${state.docMetadata?.title || 
'Report'} (Revision ${revNum})`;
          el.previewMeta.textContent = `Saved on ${new 
Date(rev.created_at).toLocaleString()} · ${rev.content.length.toLocaleString()} 
characters`;
    
          // Render line-numbered preview
          const lines = rev.content.split('\n');
          let previewHtml = '<div class="preview-line-container">';
          for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
            const escaped = editorView._escapeHtml(lines[i]);
            previewHtml += `
              <div class="preview-line">
                <span class="preview-line-num">${i + 1}</span>
                <span class="preview-line-text">${escaped || '&nbsp;'}</span>
              </div>
            `;
          }
          previewHtml += '</div>';
          el.previewContent.innerHTML = previewHtml;
    
          el.previewModal.classList.remove('hidden');
        } catch (err) {
          showToast(`Failed to preview revision: ${err.message}`, 'error');
        }
      }
    
      async function restoreRevision(revNum) {
        try {
          const rev = await Api.getRevisionContent(state.currentDocId, revNum);
          const beforeText = docModel.getText();
          const beforeSelections = 
JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(editorView.selections));
    
          docModel.setText(rev.content);
          editorView.setSelections([{ anchor: { line: 0, col: 0 }, head: { line:
0, col: 0 } }]);
    
          // Record restore as an unsaved Undo action
          undoManager.recordAction({
            beforeText,
            afterText: rev.content,
            beforeSelections,
            afterSelections: editorView.selections,
            type: 'restore_revision',
            forceNewGroup: true
          });
    
          updateDirtyState();
          updateDocumentStats();
          editorView.render();
          editorView.focus();
    
          if (!el.previewModal.classList.contains('hidden')) {
            el.previewModal.classList.add('hidden');
          }
    
          showToast(`Revision ${revNum} restored as an unsaved edit. Press 
Ctrl+Z to undo or Save to commit.`, 'info', 4500);
        } catch (err) {
          showToast(`Failed to restore revision: ${err.message}`, 'error');
        }
      }
    
      // Find and Replace Controls
      function toggleFindPanel(show) {
        if (show === undefined) {
          show = el.findPanel.classList.contains('hidden');
        }
        if (show) {
          el.findPanel.classList.remove('hidden');
          // If there's selected text in editor, populate find input
          const selected = 
docModel.getSelectedText(editorView.getPrimarySelection());
          if (selected && !selected.includes('\n')) {
            el.findInput.value = selected;
          }
          el.findInput.focus();
          el.findInput.select();
          updateFindMatches();
        } else {
          el.findPanel.classList.add('hidden');
          findEngine.setQuery('');
          editorView.setSearchHighlights([], -1);
          editorView.focus();
        }
      }
    
      function updateFindMatches() {
        const query = el.findInput.value;
        const caseSensitive = el.caseSensitiveCheckbox.checked;
        findEngine.setQuery(query, caseSensitive);
        const matches = findEngine.updateMatches(docModel);
    
        if (!query) {
          el.matchCount.textContent = '';
          editorView.setSearchHighlights([], -1);
          return;
        }
    
        if (matches.length === 0) {
          el.matchCount.textContent = 'No matches';
          editorView.setSearchHighlights([], -1);
          return;
        }
    
        // Determine nearest match
        const primary = editorView.getPrimarySelection();
        findEngine.currentIndex = findEngine.findNearestMatchIndex(primary.head,
docModel);
        const current = findEngine.getCurrentMatch();
    
        el.matchCount.textContent = `${findEngine.currentIndex + 1} of 
${matches.length}`;
        editorView.setSearchHighlights(matches, findEngine.currentIndex);
      }
    
      function jumpToCurrentMatch() {
        const match = findEngine.getCurrentMatch();
        if (!match) return;
        el.matchCount.textContent = `${findEngine.currentIndex + 1} of 
${findEngine.matches.length}`;
        editorView.setSelections([{ anchor: match.start, head: match.end }]);
        editorView.setSearchHighlights(findEngine.matches, 
findEngine.currentIndex);
        editorView.scrollToCursor();
      }
    
      // Find Event Listeners
      el.btnFind.addEventListener('click', () => toggleFindPanel());
      el.btnCloseFind.addEventListener('click', () => toggleFindPanel(false));
    
      el.findInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
        updateFindMatches();
        if (findEngine.matches.length > 0) {
          jumpToCurrentMatch();
        }
      });
    
      el.caseSensitiveCheckbox.addEventListener('change', () => {
        updateFindMatches();
        if (findEngine.matches.length > 0) {
          jumpToCurrentMatch();
        }
      });
    
      el.findInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (e.shiftKey) {
            findEngine.prevMatch();
          } else {
            findEngine.nextMatch();
          }
          jumpToCurrentMatch();
        } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
          e.preventDefault();
          // "When the Find input has focus, Escape should return focus to the 
editing area without changing the current document selection"
          editorView.focus();
        }
      });
    
      el.replaceInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
          e.preventDefault();
          replaceCurrentMatch();
        } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
          e.preventDefault();
          editorView.focus();
        }
      });
    
      el.btnFindNext.addEventListener('click', () => {
        findEngine.nextMatch();
        jumpToCurrentMatch();
      });
    
      el.btnFindPrev.addEventListener('click', () => {
        findEngine.prevMatch();
        jumpToCurrentMatch();
      });
    
      function replaceCurrentMatch() {
        findEngine.setReplaceText(el.replaceInput.value);
        const res = findEngine.replaceCurrent(docModel);
        if (!res) return;
    
        undoManager.recordAction({
          beforeText: res.beforeText,
          afterText: res.afterText,
          beforeSelections: [res.selection],
          afterSelections: [res.selection],
          type: 'replace',
          forceNewGroup: true
        });
    
        updateDirtyState();
        updateDocumentStats();
        jumpToCurrentMatch();
        editorView.render();
      }
    
      function replaceAllMatches() {
        findEngine.setReplaceText(el.replaceInput.value);
        const res = findEngine.replaceAll(docModel);
        if (res.count === 0) {
          showToast('No matches to replace', 'info');
          return;
        }
    
        undoManager.recordAction({
          beforeText: res.beforeText,
          afterText: res.afterText,
          beforeSelections: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(editorView.selections)),
          afterSelections: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(editorView.selections)),
          type: 'replace_all',
          forceNewGroup: true
        });
    
        updateDirtyState();
        updateDocumentStats();
        updateFindMatches();
        editorView.render();
        showToast(`Replaced ${res.count} occurrence(s)`, 'success');
      }
    
      el.btnReplaceCurrent.addEventListener('click', replaceCurrentMatch);
      el.btnReplaceAll.addEventListener('click', replaceAllMatches);
    
      // Toolbar & General Action Listeners
      el.btnSave.addEventListener('click', () => saveCurrentReport());
      el.btnUndo.addEventListener('click', () => editorView.performUndo());
      el.btnRedo.addEventListener('click', () => editorView.performRedo());
    
      el.btnHistory.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isHidden = el.historySidebar.classList.contains('hidden');
        if (isHidden) {
          refreshRevisionsList();
          el.historySidebar.classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
          el.historySidebar.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      });
    
      el.btnCloseHistory.addEventListener('click', () => {
        el.historySidebar.classList.add('hidden');
      });
    
      // Report Switcher
      el.reportSelect.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        const selectedId = e.target.value;
        if (state.isDirty) {
          if (!confirm('You have unsaved changes in the current report. Switch 
report anyway and discard unsaved edits?')) {
            e.target.value = state.currentDocId;
            return;
          }
        }
        loadDocument(selectedId);
      });
    
      // Conflict Modal Actions
      el.btnConflictKeep.addEventListener('click', () => {
        el.conflictModal.classList.add('hidden');
        showToast('Keeping unsaved draft in editor. You may review and save when
ready.', 'info', 3500);
        editorView.focus();
      });
    
      el.btnConflictPreview.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        el.conflictModal.classList.add('hidden');
        try {
          const doc = await Api.getReport(state.currentDocId);
          previewRevision(doc.current_revision);
        } catch (err) {
          showToast('Failed to fetch server revision for preview', 'error');
        }
      });
    
      el.btnConflictReload.addEventListener('click', () => {
        el.conflictModal.classList.add('hidden');
        loadDocument(state.currentDocId);
      });
    
      // Preview Modal Actions
      el.btnClosePreview.addEventListener('click', () => {
        el.previewModal.classList.add('hidden');
        editorView.focus();
      });
    
      el.btnRestorePreview.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (state.previewRevision) {
          restoreRevision(state.previewRevision.revision_number);
        }
      });
    
      // Initial Load
      await loadReportsList();
      await loadDocument('incident-alpha');
    });

+384|    if (show) {
+385|      el.findPanel.classList.remove('hidden');
+386|      // If there's selected text in editor, populate find input
+387|      const selected = 
docModel.getSelectedText(editorView.getPrimarySelection());
+388|      if (selected && !selected.includes('\n')) {
+389|        el.findInput.value = selected;
+390|      }
+391|      el.findInput.focus();
+392|      el.findInput.select();
+393|      updateFindMatches();
+394|    } else {
+395|      el.findPanel.classList.add('hidden');
+396|      findEngine.setQuery('');
+397|      editorView.setSearchHighlights([], -1);
+398|      editorView.focus();
+399|    }
+400|  }
+401|
+402|  function updateFindMatches() {
+403|    const query = el.findInput.value;
+404|    const caseSensitive = el.caseSensitiveCheckbox.checked;
+405|    findEngine.setQuery(query, caseSensitive);
+406|    const matches = findEngine.updateMatches(docModel);
+407|
+408|    if (!query) {
+409|      el.matchCount.textContent = '';
+410|      editorView.setSearchHighlights([], -1);
+411|      return;
+412|    }
+413|
+414|    if (matches.length === 0) {
+415|      el.matchCount.textContent = 'No matches';
+416|      editorView.setSearchHighlights([], -1);
+417|      return;
+418|    }
+419|
+420|    // Determine nearest match
+421|    const primary = editorView.getPrimarySelection();
+422|    findEngine.currentIndex = 
findEngine.findNearestMatchIndex(primary.head, docModel);
+423|    const current = findEngine.getCurrentMatch();
+424|
+425|    el.matchCount.textContent = `${findEngine.currentIndex + 1} of 
${matches.length}`;
+426|    editorView.setSearchHighlights(matches, findEngine.currentIndex);
+427|  }
+428|
+429|  function jumpToCurrentMatch() {
+430|    const match = findEngine.getCurrentMatch();
+431|    if (!match) return;
+432|    el.matchCount.textContent = `${findEngine.currentIndex + 1} of 
${findEngine.matches.length}`;
+433|    editorView.setSelections([{ anchor: match.start, head: match.end }]);
+434|    editorView.setSearchHighlights(findEngine.matches, 
findEngine.currentIndex);
+435|    editorView.scrollToCursor();
+436|  }
+437|
+438|  // Find Event Listeners
+439|  el.btnFind.addEventListener('click', () => toggleFindPanel());
+440|  el.btnCloseFind.addEventListener('click', () => toggleFindPanel(false));
+441|
+442|  el.findInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
+443|    updateFindMatches();
+444|    if (findEngine.matches.length > 0) {
+445|      jumpToCurrentMatch();
+446|    }
+447|  });
+448|
+449|  el.caseSensitiveCheckbox.addEventListener('change', () => {
+450|    updateFindMatches();
+451|    if (findEngine.matches.length > 0) {
+452|      jumpToCurrentMatch();
+453|    }
+454|  });
+455|
+456|  el.findInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+457|    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
+458|      e.preventDefault();
+459|      if (e.shiftKey) {
+460|        findEngine.prevMatch();
+461|      } else {
+462|        findEngine.nextMatch();
+463|      }
+464|      jumpToCurrentMatch();
+465|    } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
+466|      e.preventDefault();
+467|      // "When the Find input has focus, Escape should return focus to the 
editing area without changing the current document selection"
+468|      editorView.focus();
+469|    }
+470|  });
+471|
+472|  el.replaceInput.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+473|    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
+474|      e.preventDefault();
+475|      replaceCurrentMatch();
+476|    } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
+477|      e.preventDefault();
+478|      editorView.focus();
+479|    }
+480|  });
+481|
+482|  el.btnFindNext.addEventListener('click', () => {
+483|    findEngine.nextMatch();
+484|    jumpToCurrentMatch();
+485|  });
+486|
+487|  el.btnFindPrev.addEventListener('click', () => {
+488|    findEngine.prevMatch();
+489|    jumpToCurrentMatch();
+490|  });
+491|
+492|  function replaceCurrentMatch() {
+493|    findEngine.setReplaceText(el.replaceInput.value);
+494|    const res = findEngine.replaceCurrent(docModel);
+495|    if (!res) return;
+496|
+497|    undoManager.recordAction({
+498|      beforeText: res.beforeText,
+499|      afterText: res.afterText,
+500|      beforeSelections: [res.selection],
+501|      afterSelections: [res.selection],
+502|      type: 'replace',
+503|      forceNewGroup: true
+504|    });
+505|
+506|    updateDirtyState();
+507|    updateDocumentStats();
+508|    jumpToCurrentMatch();
+509|    editorView.render();
+510|  }
+511|
+512|  function replaceAllMatches() {
+513|    findEngine.setReplaceText(el.replaceInput.value);
+514|    const res = findEngine.replaceAll(docModel);
+515|    if (res.count === 0) {
+516|      showToast('No matches to replace', 'info');
+517|      return;
+518|    }
+519|
+520|    undoManager.recordAction({
+521|      beforeText: res.beforeText,
+522|      afterText: res.afterText,
+523|      beforeSelections: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(editorView.selections)),
+524|      afterSelections: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(editorView.selections)),
+525|      type: 'replace_all',
+526|      forceNewGroup: true
+527|    });
+528|
+529|    updateDirtyState();
+530|    updateDocumentStats();
+531|    updateFindMatches();
+532|    editorView.render();
+533|    showToast(`Replaced ${res.count} occurrence(s)`, 'success');
+534|  }
+535|
+536|  el.btnReplaceCurrent.addEventListener('click', replaceCurrentMatch);
+537|  el.btnReplaceAll.addEventListener('click', replaceAllMatches);
+538|
+539|  // Toolbar & General Action Listeners

+543|

Reasoning:
**My Design for PatchPad's Front-End**

Okay, time to get into the nitty-gritty and design the `index.html` and 
`style.css` files. Let's make sure we're on the same page and review the core 
requirements from `interface.md`. We need a user-friendly editor, and that 
means: it needs to function well in a standard browser, with the report title, 
editing surface, save state, revision history, cursor position, and clear 
feedback for successes and failures all visible and easily understood. The 
“Northwind API Incident Report” title needs to remain persistently visible so 
users know what they are editing. Discoverability is key: Save, Undo, Redo, Find
Next, Replace Current, Replace All, and the revision history must be easy to 
find. Labels, keyboard focus, errors, and history entries need to be readable 
and consistent. No hidden routes or magic IDs here! Everything must be clear and
intuitive, without separate instructions. And, importantly, a polished visual 
design – readable typography, good contrast, consistent spacing, clear 
hierarchy, and matching component styling throughout the interface, including 
revision history entries and previews. Finally, it must be responsive at a 
1280x800 desktop size, with controls and history that can wrap, and report lines
and previews that scroll as needed within their own areas, avoiding any 
page-wide overflow, clipped controls, or overlapping text. We're aiming for a 
single, well-designed theme.

With that in mind, I'm envisioning a clean, modern, high-contrast, accessible, 
and polished interface. I'm going to start laying out the HTML structure in 
`index.html`, and style it in `style.css`. Here's the structure I have in mind:

First, an **App Bar/Header**:
*   With a Brand Logo, labeled as "PatchPad".
*   Displaying the Document Title ("Northwind API Incident Report") and relevant
Metadata (like "Riley Stone").
*   A Report selector dropdown is needed for switching between server-backed 
reports.
*   A Toolbar with these clearly labeled buttons:
    *   Save, showing the save status as a badge
    *   Undo
    *   Redo
    *   Find & Replace
    *   Revision History

Then, a **Find & Replace Bar**:
*   With an input field for Find
*   An input field for Replace
*   A match count indicator
*   Prev, Next, Replace, Replace All, and Close buttons.
*   A Match Case checkbox.

The **Main Content Area** will be next:
*   With an Editor Container (a custom DOM surface, for editing the content)
*   A Revision History Drawer (a collapsible sidebar that expands from the 
right).

And, at the bottom, a **Status Bar**:
*   Line & Column display (`Ln 1, Col 1`)
*   Character and line count.
*   The current revision badge
*   And Save Status (`✓ Saved` or `● Unsaved changes` or `⚠ Conflict`).

Finally, I'll need to account for a few **Modals/Overlays**:
*   A Preview Modal (for previewing older revisions with full line numbers and 
text).
*   A Conflict Modal (for handling 409 save conflicts).
*   And a Toast notifications container for success and error messages.

Alright, time to get coding! First, let's write `/app/public/index.html`.

Reasoning:
Here's my summary, as I see it:

**CSS Strategy for PatchPad**

Okay, time to build that `style.css` file. I need to make sure the visual design
is spot-on for PatchPad. The goal is to build a stylesheet that achieves clean, 
modern, accessible aesthetics. That means a strong emphasis on high-contrast 
typography, a clear visual hierarchy, and a responsive layout that'll look good 
on various screen sizes. I'm leaning towards a dark or neutral theme, and I'll 
need to choose some crisp colors that really stand out for active elements, like
carets, selections, search highlights, and those elements in the UI like gutter 
numbers, toolbar buttons, and modals.

Now, let me make sure I'm keeping the design requirements from `interface.md` in
mind. I need to ensure a consistent look and feel across the application – 
readable typography, clear contrast, and spacing, and a consistent hierarchy. I 
need to make sure this styling carries through the history entries and previews 
too. I need a consistent design across the component styling.

The layout should remain readable at 1280x800 desktop size. I'll make sure the 
controls and history can wrap if necessary. Long lines and previews should 
scroll within their respective areas to avoid page-wide overflow, clipped 
controls, and overlapping text. Remember, a single, well-designed theme will be 
sufficient. Time to get coding! I'm going to start writing 
`/app/public/css/style.css` now.

Document & Reports\n- `GET /`: Serves the PatchPad editor browser client.\n- 
`GET /api/reports`: Lists available reports with metadata (`id`, `title`, 
`author`, `summary`, `current_revision`, `updated_at`).\n- `GET 
/api/reports/:id`: Loads a specific report with its latest saved content, 
revision number, and metadata.\n\n### Saving & Conflict Safety\n- `POST 
/api/reports/:id/save`: Saves report changes.\n  - Accepts JSON payload: `{ 
baseRevision: integer, content: string, documentId?: string, comment?: string 
}`.\n  - Performs conflict detection: returns HTTP `409 Conflict` if 
`baseRevision` does not match the server's current revision.\n  - Unchanged 
saves do not increment the revision.\n  - Changed saves increment the revision 
by 1 and store a new snapshot in SQLite.\n- `PUT /api/reports/:id`: Alias for 
saving report changes.\n\n### Revision History & Previews\n- `GET 
/api/reports/:id/revisions`: Lists all revisions for a report with revision 
numbers, timestamps, character counts, and comments.\n- `GET 
/api/reports/:id/revisions/:revNum`: Retrieves the full content and metadata of 
a specific revision for previewing or restoring.\n"}

**Manifest Creation**

`/app/package.json`. Let me double-check the specifics in the `overview.md` 
document, just to be absolutely certain. Right, it says I need to include the 
start command, the SQLite path, and the main application routes, along with 
their request methods, that are used for tasks like opening reports, saving 
data, and browsing revision history. It's important to remember that a single 
route can handle multiple purposes – no need to overcomplicate things with 
separate endpoints for every single data read. For the database path, it has to 
be on its own line in the format `SQLite path: /app/your-file.db`, using the 
full absolute path under `/app`. The path can be in backticks, and the filename 
itself doesn't have any specific extension requirement. And, crucial point, a 
fresh database should trigger a seed recreation, while normal restarts are 
designed to preserve existing data.

    # PatchPad App Manifest
    
    Start command: `npm start`
    SQLite path: `/app/patchpad.db`
    
    ## Main Application Routes and Methods
    
    ### Document & Reports
    - `GET /`: Serves the PatchPad editor browser client.
    - `GET /api/reports`: Lists available reports with metadata (`id`, `title`, 
`author`, `summary`, `current_revision`, `updated_at`).
    - `GET /api/reports/:id`: Loads a specific report with its latest saved 
content, revision number, and metadata.
    
    ### Saving & Conflict Safety
    - `POST /api/reports/:id/save`: Saves report changes.
      - Accepts JSON payload: `{ baseRevision: integer, content: string, 
documentId?: string, comment?: string }`.
      - Performs conflict detection: returns HTTP `409 Conflict` if 
`baseRevision` does not match the server's current revision.
      - Unchanged saves do not increment the revision.
      - Changed saves increment the revision by 1 and store a new snapshot in 
SQLite.
    - `PUT /api/reports/:id`: Alias for saving report changes.
    
    ### Revision History & Previews
    - `GET /api/reports/:id/revisions`: Lists all revisions for a report with 
revision numbers, timestamps, character counts, and comments.
    - `GET /api/reports/:id/revisions/:revNum`: Retrieves the full content and 
metadata of a specific revision for previewing or restoring.

});\n          } catch (e) {\n            resolve({ status: res.statusCode,

Reasoning:
**My Test Suite Strategy**

Okay, here's the plan. I'm going to build a really thorough test suite within 
`/app/test/` to make sure this whole system is rock solid. I need to be 
absolutely confident that every part works as expected.

First, I'll start with the **Seed Document Loader & Content Verification**. I'll
confirm that the initial state loads correctly from revision 1, keeping the 
title "Northwind API Incident Report," authored by "Riley Stone," with the 
correct number of lines – 1226 – and crucially, the sentinel line 
`OMEGA-END-ANCHOR`. I need to verify that restarting doesn't mess up the saved 
content or duplicate the seed data.

Next, I'll focus on the **Server API & Validation**. I'll test the endpoints 
thoroughly: `GET /api/reports` should return the right list of reports, and `GET
/api/reports/incident-alpha` needs to deliver the correct details and content. I
need to explicitly check all the error conditions: stale saves (409), invalid 
revision numbers (400), non-text content (400), document ID mismatches (400), 
and unknown documents (404). I'll also confirm that unchanged saves return a 200
without creating new revisions, and changed saves return a 200 and correctly 
increment the revision.

Then, there's the **Revision History**. I'll test that `GET 
/api/reports/incident-alpha/revisions` lists all revisions in the expected 
order, and that `GET /api/reports/incident-alpha/revisions/1` correctly 
retrieves the content of specific revisions.

A critical part will be the **Document Model & Text Engine**. I need to ensure 
that line splitting and text reconstruction are flawless. I'll meticulously test
grapheme cluster handling, especially multi-byte characters, emoji, and 
combining accents. I also need to verify that cursor navigation and deletion 
work correctly with those. Furthermore, the navigation behaviors for word 
boundaries and selection normalization need comprehensive testing. Indentation 
features, specifically, block indent and outdent with all the subtle edge cases,
are going to get tested to ensure proper behavior. The edge case "A selection 
ending at the start of the next line does not include that next line" needs 
specific validation.

The **Find & Replace Engine** needs its own thorough section. I'll test match 
counting, proper next/prev match navigation with wrapping, replacing current 
matches, and the "Replace All" functionality to confirm it works in an atomic 
and efficient manner.

Finally, the **Undo / Redo Engine** is crucial for user experience. I need to 
verify: Coalesced typing that it all undoes as one action; cursor movements or 
other distinct actions start new steps, and that Replace All, block indent, and 
revision restore all function correctly as undo steps. Additionally, I need to 
confirm that a new edit clears the redo stack.

I'll put all of this in `/app/test/run-tests.js` to run the unit and integration
tests! This needs to be comprehensive.

    const assert = require('assert');
    const fs = require('fs');
    const path = require('path');
    const http = require('http');
